Sub Raporla1
	Set SF321E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC321E")
	MpsNo = Trim(SF321E.MPSNO)
	IsNoDegeri = SF321E.JOBNO
	If Trim(IsNoDegeri) = "" Then
		Msgbox "Ýþ No Alaný Boþ..."
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "FORM_ISLISTESI", SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, Par
	
	Set S32E = Doc.Select1("dsdasda", "SFDC32E", "JOBNO='"&IsNoDegeri&"'", "RECTARIH1+RECTIME1", -1)
	S32E.RefResh
	KayitSayisi = S32E.GetRecCount
	
	If KayitSayisi = 0 Then 
		Msgbox "Kayýt Bulunamadý..."
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")	
	MTable.Empty
	
	For i = 1 To 	KayitSayisi
		satirNo = MTable.AddRow()
		MTable.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

		IsDurumu = Trim(S32E.D7_AKSIYON(i))
		EvrakNo = Trim(S32E.EVRAKNO(i))
		'CalBilOlusturan = Trim(GetCalBilOlusturan(EvrakNo))
		Set S32O = Doc.Select1("ds11sda", "SFDC32O", "EVRAKNO='"&EvrakNo&"'", "", -1)
		If S32O.GetRecCount > 0 Then
			Operator = S32O.OPERATOR(1)
			OperatorAdi = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERS00", "AD", Operator, ""))
		End If
		
		MTable.EVRAKNO = EvrakNo
		MTable.JOBNO = Trim(S32E.JOBNO(i))
		MTable.RECTARIH1 = S32E.RECTARIH1(i)
		MTable.RECTIME1 = S32E.RECTIME1(i)
		MTable.RECTARIH2 = S32E.RECTARIH2(i)
		MTable.RECTIME2 = S32E.RECTIME2(i)
		MTable.CREATER = OperatorAdi
		
		If IsDurumu <> "" Then 
			MTable.D7_AKSIYONX = IsDurumu
		Else
			MTable.D7_AKSIYONX = ""
		End If 
		MTable.SF_MIKTAR = S32E.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MTable.STOK_CODE = S32E.STOK_CODE(i)
	Next
	MTable.Refresh
	MTable.SetCurrentRow(1)

End Sub
'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Makro1()
	Set WIZARD_STACK = Doc.GetTableObject("WIZARD_STACK")
	WIZARD_STACK.AddField2 "D_ID2", "CHAR", 30, 0, L, "", ""
	WIZARD_STACK.Setd7FieldEvent "D_ID2","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "D_ID2_AV"
	
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLE=SFDC32E(NOFILE)","IO")
	MTableT.AddField2 "D7_AKSIYONX", "CHAR", 1, 0, L, "", ""
	MTableT.AddField2 "CREATER", "CHAR", 50, 0, L, "", ""
End Sub
'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub D_ID2_AV()
	Set WIZARD_STACK = Doc.GetTableObject("WIZARD_STACK")
	Barkod = WIZARD_STACK.D_ID2
	Barkod = UCase(Barkod)
	Barkod = Replace(Barkod, "*", "-")
	Barkod = Replace(Barkod, ".", "%")
	Barkod = Replace(Barkod, "Ç", "%")
	Barkod = Replace(Barkod, "ç", "%")

	WIZARD_STACK.D_ID = Barkod
	Set Par=Doc.CreateSObject("Parameters")

	Par.SetParameter "WZ_TABLENAME", "WIZARD_STACK"
	Par.SetParameter "WZ_FIELDNAME","D_ID"
	Par.SetParameter "WZ_INDEX", -1

	Doc.runcommand1 "CMD_WIZARD_01", par
	WIZARD_STACK.D_ID2 = ""
End Sub
'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function GetCalBilOlusturan(EvrakNo)
	QStr1 = "EVRAKNO='SFDC32:"+trim(EvrakNo)+"' & PROCESS='C'"
	Set U00 = Doc.Select1("XXX1", "ULOG00", QStr1, "", -1)
	if U00.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetCalBilOlusturan = Trim(U00.USERNAME(1))
	Else
		GetCalBilOlusturan = ""
	End if
	Doc.DeleteTableObject("XXX1")
End Function

Makro Kodu : HARCAMA
Hangi Tablo için : MUHA10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Harcama Fiþlerini Mahsup Fiþine Ýþleme
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()

	Doc.StatusBarMessage "Personel Harcama Formu Acýldý Bilgiler Mahsup Fiþine Aktarýlýyor"
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb = Excel.Workbooks.Open("C:\harcama\Harcama.xlsx")
	Set Sh1 = Wb.Sheets(1)
	MUHA20T.SafePack
	

		for  i = 2 To 6000	
			if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) <> "" Then			
				satirNo = MUHA20T.AddRow()
				MUHA20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)		
				MUHA20T.KOD = Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value
				MUHA20T.ACIKLAMA = Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value
				MUHA20T.BORCFIYAT = Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value
				MUHA20T.OR_EVRAKSIRANO = Sh1.Cells(i,5).Value
				MUHA20T.OR_EVRAKTARIHI = Sh1.Cells(i,6).Value
				MUHA20T.MAL_V_HIZMETCINSI = Sh1.Cells(i,7).Value	
				MUHA20T.MAL_V_HIZMETMIKTR = Sh1.Cells(i,8).Value	
				MUHA20T.CH_UNVAN = Sh1.Cells(i,9).Value	
				'msgbox Sh1.Cells(3).Value
				if Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value <= 0 Then MUHA20T.ALACAKFIYAT = Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value
				if Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value <= 0 Then MUHA20T.BORCFIYAT = Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value
			else 	
				Excel.Quit
				exit sub
			end if 
		next 

	Excel.Quit
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu

Kod : 
Function GetTableHTML(Table, ByVal HTMLBaslik, TabloBaslikArray)
	HTMLBaslik = Replace(HTMLBaslik, vbcrlf, "<br>")
	FieldArr = Table.GetFields()

	HTMLStr = ""
	HTMLStr = "<html  lang='tr-TR'><body>" & HTMLBaslik
	HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<table border=1 cellspacing=0 cellpadding=2>"
	HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<tr>"
	For each Baslik in TabloBaslikArray
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<th bgcolor=#EEEEFF>" & Baslik & "</th>"
	Next
	HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "</tr>"

	For i=1 to Table.GetRecCount
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<tr>"
		For j = 0 to uBound(FieldArr,1)
			if j <= uBound(TabloBaslikArray) Then
				AlanTuru = FieldArr(j, 2)
				AlanDegeri = Table.GetFieldValue(FieldArr(j,0), i)
				Align = "left"
				if AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
					Align = "right"
				Elseif AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
					AlanDegeri = Doc.StrToNorDate(AlanDegeri)
				Elseif AlanTuru = "CHAR" Then
					AlanDegeri = trim(AlanDegeri)
				End if

				HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<td align=" & Align & ">" & AlanDegeri & "</td>"
			End if
		Next
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "</tr>"
	Next
	HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "</table></body></html>"
	GetTableHTML = HTMLStr
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DUNDAR_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S

Kod : 
Public sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic, satirAra, SatirBitis
Public ToplamEURTutar, ToplamEURIskonto, ToplamEURIskontoluTutar
Public Grid, xCizgi
Sub Makro1()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 15
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 20 
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 7
	satiryuksekligi = 6
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 210
	satirSonu = AltMargin - 30

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "BORDRO", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni,"")
	
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        

	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	
	'Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.line(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi *3,solmargin,0,sagmargin-solmargin)
	res = pe1.pensize(0)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 2,solmargin+2,satiryuksekligi,50,"Rapor Tarihi / Saati")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.gettop,solmargin+145,satiryuksekligi,20,"Sayfa No  1")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom,solmargin + 2,satiryuksekligi,50,"Ýþyeri Adý")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.gettop,solmargin+145,satiryuksekligi,20,"Dönem")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom,solmargin + 2,satiryuksekligi,50,"Bölüm Adý")
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 2,solmargin+34,satiryuksekligi,2,":")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom,solmargin+34,satiryuksekligi,2,":")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom,solmargin+34,satiryuksekligi,2,":")
	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(1)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 5,solmargin+35,satiryuksekligi,50,"KAZANÇLAR")
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 5,solmargin+125,satiryuksekligi,50,"YASAL KESÝNTÝLER")
	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(0)

	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 7,solmargin,satiryuksekligi,50,"Normal Çalýþma")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom,solmargin,satiryuksekligi,50,"Hafta Tatili")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom,solmargin,satiryuksekligi,50,"Resmi Tatil")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom,solmargin,satiryuksekligi,50,"Yýllýk Ýzin")

			HaftaTatili1 = HaftaTatili1 + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_GUN(m)
			HaftaTatili2 = HaftaTatili2 + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)

			ResmiTatil1 =ResmiTatil1 + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_GUN(m)
			ResmiTatil2 = ResmiTatil2 + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)

			UcretliIzin1 =UcretliIzin1 + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_GUN(m)

	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom,solmargin + 70,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(SosyalYardimToplami,20,2))

	for i=1 to Tbl_BRDR1TP.Getrowcount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "SSK Bilgileri Hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & Tbl_BRDR1TP2.Getrowcount()
			tarih1=Tbl_BRDR1TP.TARIH1(i)

				isegirensayisi=isegirensayisi+1
			end if

				istencikansayisi=istencikansayisi+1
			end if

			Select case trim(Cinsiyet)
				case "K"
					k_sayisi = k_sayisi + 1
				case "E"
					e_sayisi=e_sayisi + 1
			End select

		sskmatrahi=sskmatrahi + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBORDRO_SSKMATRAHI(i)
		GVMatrahi=GVMatrahi + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBORDRO_GVMATRAHI(i)
		GelirVergisi=GelirVergisi + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBORDRO_GV(i)
		SSKGun = SSKGun + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBORDRO_SSKPRIMGN_P(i)

		IsciHastalik = IsciHastalik + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBRDR_SSKPRIMIH(i)
		IsciMaluliyet = IsciMaluliyet + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBRDR_SSKPRIMIM(i)
		IsciIssizlik = IsciIssizlik + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBRDR_SSKPRIMII(i)
		IsciSGDP = IsciSGDP + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBRDR_SSKPRIMIS(i)
		IsciToplam = IsciHastalik + IsciMaluliyet
		IsciAlttoplam = IsciToplam + IsciSGDP 
		IsciGenelToplam = IsciToplam + IsciIssizlik
	
		IsvAnalik = IsvAnalik + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBRDR_SSKPRIMVA(i)
		IsvHastalik = IsvHastalik + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBRDR_SSKPRIMVH(i)
		IsvMaluliyet = IsvMaluliyet + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBRDR_SSKPRIMVM(i)
		IsvIssizlik = IsvIssizlik + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBRDR_SSKPRIMVI(i)
		IsvSGDP = IsvSGDP + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBRDR_SSKPRIMVS(i)
		IsvMeslek = IsvMeslek + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBRDR_SSKPRIMVK(i) 
		IsvToplam = IsvAnalik + IsvHastalik + IsvMaluliyet + IsvMeslek
		IsvAltToplam = IsvToplam + IsvSGDP  	
		IssizlikToplam = IsciIssizlik + IsvIssizlik
		IsvGenelToplam = IsvToplam + IsvIssizlik
		SSK_Toplam = IsciGenelToplam + IsvGenelToplam
		SGDP_Toplam = IsciSGDP + IsvSGDP
		Toplam = SSK_Toplam + SGDP_Toplam
	Next
'SSK Alanlarýn Yazýlmasý
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 7,solmargin + 142,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(SSKMatrahi,20,2))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 8,solmargin + 142,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(IsciToplam,20,2))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 9,solmargin + 142,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(IsciSGDP,20,2))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 10,solmargin + 162,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(IsciAlttoplam,20,2))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 11,solmargin + 142,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(IsvToplam,20,2))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 12,solmargin + 142,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(IsvSGDP,20,2))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 13,solmargin + 162,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(IsvAlttoplam,20,2))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 14,solmargin + 142,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(IsciIssizlik,20,2))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 15,solmargin + 142,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(IsvIssizlik,20,2))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 16,solmargin + 162,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(IssizlikToplam,20,2))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 18,solmargin + 162,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(IsvGenelToplam,20,2))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 19,solmargin + 162,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(IsciGenelToplam,20,2))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 20,solmargin + 162,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(SSK_Toplam,20,2))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 21,solmargin + 162,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(SGDP_Toplam,20,2))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 22,solmargin + 162,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(Toplam,20,2))


	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 24,solmargin + 142,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(GVMatrahi,20,2))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 25,solmargin + 142,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(GelirVergisi,20,2))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 26,solmargin + 142,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(DVergisi,20,2))

'KESÝNTÝLERÝN YAZILMASI
	For z = 1 to Tbl_BRDR1TP.Getreccount()	
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Avans/Kesinti Bilgileri Hesaplanýyor : " & z & " / " &  Tbl_BRDR1TP.Getreccount()
		PersonelKodu=Trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP.KOD(z))
		AvansArray = GetTPA("__BRDR1TPA", PersonelKodu, "A")
		BorcArray = GetTPA("__BRDR1TPA", PersonelKodu, "B")

		Avans = Avans + AvansArray(0)
		Borc = Borc + BorcArray(0)

	next

	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 29,solmargin + 50,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(Avans,20,2))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 30,solmargin + 50,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(Borc,20,2))





'Ýstatistik alanlarýnýn yazýlmasý
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 35,solmargin + 40,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(SSKGun,20,0))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 36,solmargin + 40,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(VergiGun,20,0))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 37,solmargin + 40,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(PersonelSayisi,20,0))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 38,solmargin + 40,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(e_sayisi,20,0))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 39,solmargin + 40,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(k_sayisi,20,0))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 40,solmargin + 40,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(isegirensayisi,20,0))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 41,solmargin + 40,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(istencikansayisi,20,0))

	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 35,solmargin + 95,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(NormalCalisma1,20,0) & " Gün")
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 36,solmargin + 95,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(HaftaTatili1,20,0) & " Gün")
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 37,solmargin + 95,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(ResmiTatil1,20,0) & " Gün")
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 38,solmargin + 95,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(Yillilkizin1,20,0) & " Gün")
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 39,solmargin + 95,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(UcretliIzin1,20,0) & " Gün")
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 40,solmargin + 95,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(DogumIzni1,20,0) & " Gün")
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 41,solmargin + 95,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(UcretsizIzin1,20,0) & " Gün")

	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 35,solmargin + 150,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(Mesai50_1,20,0) & " Saat")
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 36,solmargin + 150,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(Mesai100_1,20,0) & " Saat")
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 37,solmargin + 150,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(MesaiToplami2,20,0) & " Saat")
 
End Sub
Function GetTPA(Tablo, PersonelKodu, TPAType)
	Dim TPAArray(1)
	TPAArray(0) = 0
	TPAArray(1) = 0
	Set TPA = Doc.Select2("WEQE", Tablo, "KOD='"+PersonelKodu+"'&ODEMETURU='"+TPAType+"'", "", -1)
	TPAArray(1) = TPA.GetRowCount()
	if TPA.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		TPA.GroupBy("KOD")
		TPAArray(0) = TPA.OR_BORCFIYAT(1)
	End if
	GetTPA = TPAArray
	xret= doc.DeleteTableObject("WEQE")
End Function

Option Explicit

Dim FileName
Dim SheetName
Dim FileName2
Dim SheetName2

const xlAutomatic=-4105
const xlCenter=-4108
const xlLeft=-4131
const xlRight=-4152
const xlContext=-5002
const xlContinuous=1
const xlThin=2

const xlEdgeBottom=9
const xlEdgeLeft=7
const xlEdgeRight=10
const xlEdgeTop=8

FileName="C:\Listeler\Ihrac Kayitli Satis Faturalari\Ihrac Kayitli Satis Fatura Listesi.xls"
SheetName="Ihrac Kayitli Satis Faturalari"

Sub Makro1()
	
	Dim i
	Dim Excel
	Dim Wb
	Dim Sh
	
	Dim cpt
	cpt=Doc.Customp_Gets("EBYNDOSD")
	cpt=Trim(cpt)
	if cpt<>"" then
		FileName=RePlace(FileName,"C:\Listeler\",cpt)
		if CreateIfNotExistDir(cpt+"Ihrac Kayitli Satis Faturalari") then
			Exit Sub
		end if
	else
		if CreateIfNotExistDir("C:\Listeler") then
			Exit Sub
		end if
		if CreateIfNotExistDir("C:\Listeler\Ihrac Kayitli Satis Faturalari") then
			Exit Sub
		end if
	end if
	
	Doc.StatusbarMessage "Excel Açiliyor"
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb = Excel.Workbooks.Add
	Set Sh = Wb.Sheets(1)
	Sh.Name = SheetName
	
	Dim DeleteSheets()
	ReDim DeleteSheets(Wb.Sheets.Count)
	
	For i = 1 To Wb.Sheets.Count
		If Trim(Wb.Sheets(i).Name) <> SheetName Then
			DeleteSheets(i) = Wb.Sheets(i).Name
		End If
	Next
	
	For i = 1 To UBound(DeleteSheets)
		If Trim(DeleteSheets(i)) <> "" Then
			Wb.Sheets(DeleteSheets(i)).Delete
		End If
	Next
	
	Call SetHeader(Sh)
	Call SetData(Sh)
	
	Call DeleteFile(FileName)
	
	Excel.ActiveWorkbook.SaveAs FileName
	
	Excel.Quit
	Set Excel=nothing
	
	Doc.d7MsgBox "Oluþturulan Dosya-> "+FileName,"Freedom",MB_OK
	
	Dim tbl_EBEYNM2E
	Set tbl_EBEYNM2E=Doc.GetTableObject("EBEYNM2E")
	tbl_EBEYNM2E.XLSFILENAME1=FileName
	
End Sub
Sub SetHeader(Sh)
	
	Call WriteCel(Sh,2, 8, 18, 3.71,  "Arial", 14, True,False,xlCenter,"","ÝHRAÇ KAYITLI SATIÞ FATURASI LiSTESÝ")
	
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 2, 76.75, 3.71,  "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Sýra No")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 3, 76.75, 11.86, "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Ýhraç Kayýtlý Satýþ Faturasýnýn Tarihi")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 4, 76.75, 7.86,  "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Ýhraç Kayýtlý Satýþ Faturasýnýn Serisi")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 5, 76.75, 10.29, "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Ýhraç Kayýtlý Satýþ Faturasýnýn Sira No'su")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 6, 76.75, 20, "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Alýcýnýn Adý-Soyadý / Ünvaný")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 7, 76.75, 14,  "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Alýcýnýn Vergi Kimlik Numarasý / TC Kimlik Numarasý")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 8, 76.75, 16.29, "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Malýn Cinsi")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 9, 76.75, 11.14, "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Malýn Miktarý")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4,10, 76.75, 12,  "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Malýn KDV Hariç Tutarý")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4,11, 76.75, 8.43,  "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Malýn KDV'si")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4,12, 76.75, 15,  "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Ýhracatçý Tarafýndan Yurt Dýþýna Düzenlenen Satýþ Faturasýnýn Tarihi(GÇB Üzerindeki Bilgiler)")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4,13, 76.75, 13.57, "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","GÇB Tescil No")
End Sub

Sub SetData(Sh)
	Dim i
	Dim tbl_EBEYNM21
	Dim FT_IVD_KH_TOPLAM
	Dim FT_KDV_TUTARI
	Dim Qstr,S70T
	Dim Donembasi,donemsonu
	' Set tbl_EBEYNM21=Doc.GetTableObject("EBEYNM21")
	
	Donembasi = "01"& "/"& EBEYNM2E.IDB_AY &"/"& EBEYNM2E.IDB_YIL
	Donembasi = Doc.AyinilkGunu(Donembasi)
	Donemsonu = Doc.AyinSonGunu(Donembasi)
	
	Set tbl_EBEYNM21 = Doc.Select1G("ds1224","STOK70T","NITELIK='XK' & TARIH >= '"& Donembasi  &"' & TARIH <= '"& Donemsonu &"'","","EVRAKNO",-1)
	
	'    QStr = "SELECT STOK70T.* FROM STOK70T"
	'    QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK70E ON STOK70T.EVRAKNO=STOK70E.EVRAKNO"
	'    QStr = QStr & " WHERE STOK70E.NITELIK='XK'"
	'    QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY EVRAKNO"
	
	'    Set S70T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("vedrg35h35hgtfhg", QStr)
	
	Dim tbl_EBEYNM2A,tbl_EBYNM222
	Set tbl_EBEYNM2A=Doc.GetTableObject("EBEYNM2A")
	Dim ftserino,ftsirano,chkodu, chkoduadi,vergino,KDVharic,KDV,gcbtarih,gcbno,KDVharic_TOPLAM, KDV_TUTARI
	Dim miktar,zz,sira_no,tanim,gcb1,gcb2,zzz,faturagosterimsayisi
	
	sira_no = 0
	
	for i=1 to tbl_EBEYNM21.GetRecCount()
		tbl_EBEYNM21.SetCurrentRow i
		
		faturagosterimsayisi = 1
		gcb1 = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK70E",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"GCBN",0)
		gcb2 = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK70E",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"GCBNA",0)
		
		if trim(gcb1) = "" then
			faturagosterimsayisi = 1
		Elseif trim(gcb1) <> "" and trim(gcb2) = "" then
			faturagosterimsayisi = 2
		Elseif trim(gcb2) <> "" then
			faturagosterimsayisi = 3
		End if
		
		for zzz=1 to faturagosterimsayisi
			
			'  sira_no = i
			sira_no = sira_no + 1
			
			ftserino = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","OR_FATURASERINO",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")
			ftsirano = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","OR_FATURASIRANO",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")
			chkodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","CH_KODU",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")
			chkoduadi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","AD",chkodu,"")
			vergino = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","VERGINO",chkodu,"")
			miktar=""
			KDVharic = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","TL_IVD_KH_TOPLAM",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")
			KDV = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","TL_KDVLERTOPLAM",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")

			if zzz=1 then
				gcbtarih = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","GCBTARIHI",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")
				gcbtarih = Doc.StrToNorDate(gcbtarih)
				gcbno = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","GCBNO",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")
			elseif zzz=2 then
				gcbno = gcb1
				gcbtarih = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK70E",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"GCBT",0)
			elseif zzz=3 then
				gcbno = gcb2
				gcbtarih = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK70E",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"GCBTA",0)
			End if

			tanim = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK70E",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"URUN",0)
			
			Doc.StatusbarMessage sira_no & ". Satir Formatlanip Yaziliyor"
			
			
			Set tbl_EBYNM222 = Doc.Select1G("d20014","STOK70T","NITELIK='XK' & EVRAKNO = '"& tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i) &"'& TARIH >= '"& Donembasi  &"' & TARIH <= '"& Donemsonu &"'","","EVRAKNO+SF_SF_UNIT",-1)
			For zz = 1 to tbl_EBYNM222.GetRecCount()
				miktar = miktar &" "& tbl_EBYNM222.SF_MIKTAR(zz) &" "& tbl_EBYNM222.SF_SF_UNIT(zz)
			Next
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4, 2, 12, 3.71, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlCenter,"@",  AlanUzunluklari(sira_no, tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"SIRANO",       "CFLAG2","L"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4, 3, 12, 11.86, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"dd.mm.yyyy",      tbl_EBEYNM21.TARIH)
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4, 4, 12, 7.86, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",   AlanUzunluklari(ftserino, tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_SERINO",       "CFLAG3","L"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4, 5, 12, 10.29, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",   AlanUzunluklari(ftsirano, tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_SIRANO",        "CFLAG4","L"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4, 6, 12, 20,  "Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",   AlanUzunluklari(chkoduadi, tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_SATICI_UNVAN",  "CFLAG5","L"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4, 7, 12, 14,  "Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",   AlanUzunluklari(vergino , tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_ARACI_FRM_VDNTCNO", "CFLAG6","L"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4, 8, 12, 16.29, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",   AlanUzunluklari(tanim,  tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_MAL_V_HIZMETCINSI", "CFLAG7","L"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4, 9, 12, 11.14, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",   AlanUzunluklari(miktar , tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_MAL_V_HIZMETMIKTR", "CFLAG8","L"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4,10, 12, 15,  "Arial", 9, True, True, xlRight,"#,##0.00", AlanUzunluklari(KDVharic , tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_IVD_KH_TOPLAM",  "CFLAG9","R"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4,11, 12, 15,  "Arial", 9, True, True, xlRight,"#,##0.00", AlanUzunluklari(KDV,  tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_KDV_TUTARI",  "CFLAG10","R"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4,12, 12, 15,  "Arial", 9, True, True, xlCenter,"dd.mm.yyyy",  AlanUzunluklari(gcbtarih , tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_TARIHI",   "CFLAG11","L"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4,13, 12, 13.57, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",   AlanUzunluklari(gcbno ,  tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_GCBNO",   "CFLAG12","L"))
		Next
		KDVharic_TOPLAM = KDVharic_TOPLAM + KDVharic
		KDV_TUTARI = KDV_TUTARI + KDV
		
	next
	
	tbl_EBEYNM21.SetCurrentRow 1
	
	FT_IVD_KH_TOPLAM=Doc.dRound(KDVharic_TOPLAM)
	FT_KDV_TUTARI=Doc.dRound(KDV_TUTARI)
	
	Dim Sum1
	Dim Sum2
	
	Sum1="=SUM(J5:J@)"
	Sum2="=SUM(K5:K@)"
	
'	Sum1=RePlace(Sum1,"J@","J"&i+4-1)
'	Sum2=RePlace(Sum2,"K@","K"&i+4-1)

	Sum1 = FT_IVD_KH_TOPLAM
	Sum2 = FT_KDV_TUTARI
	
	Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no+5,  9, 12, 11.14,"Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"","TOPLAM")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no+5, 10, 12, 15, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlRight,"#,##0.00",Sum1)
	Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no+5, 11, 12, 15, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlRight,"#,##0.00",Sum2)
	
End Sub


Function CreateIfNotExistDir(vDr)
	Dim fs
	Dim newfolder
	CreateIfNotExistDir=False
	Set fs=CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	if Not fs.FolderExists(vDr) then
		Set newfolder=fs.CreateFolder(vDr)
		if Not IsObject(newfolder) then
			CreateIfNotExistDir=TRUE
			Doc.d7MsgBox vDr + " Dizin Oluþturulamiyor.","Freedom",MB_OK
			Exit Function
		end if
	end if
	set fs=nothing
End Function

Sub WriteCel(ObjSheet,RowNum, ColNum, pRowHeight, pColumnWidth, FontName, FontSize, FontBold, pWrapText,pHorizontalAlignment,pNumberFormat,pAciklama)
	With ObjSheet.Cells(RowNum, ColNum)
	if Trim(pNumberFormat)<>"" then
		.NumberFormat = pNumberFormat
	end if
	'        .FormulaR1C1 = pAciklama
	.Formula = pAciklama
	'        .RowHeight = pRowHeight '76.75
	.HorizontalAlignment = pHorizontalAlignment 'xlCenter
	.VerticalAlignment = xlCenter
	.WrapText = pWrapText 'True
	.Orientation = 0
	.AddIndent = False
	.IndentLevel = 0
	.ShrinkToFit = False
	.ReadingOrder = xlContext
	.MergeCells = False
	.Font.Name = FontName '"Arial"
	.Font.Size = FontSize '9
	.Font.Bold = FontBold 'True
	.ColumnWidth = pColumnWidth '3.71
	End With
	With ObjSheet.Cells(RowNum, ColNum).Borders(xlEdgeLeft)
	.LineStyle = xlContinuous
	.Weight = xlThin
	.ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	End With
	With ObjSheet.Cells(RowNum, ColNum).Borders(xlEdgeTop)
	.LineStyle = xlContinuous
	.Weight = xlThin
	.ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	End With
	With ObjSheet.Cells(RowNum, ColNum).Borders(xlEdgeBottom)
	.LineStyle = xlContinuous
	.Weight = xlThin
	.ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	End With
	With ObjSheet.Cells(RowNum, ColNum).Borders(xlEdgeRight)
	.LineStyle = xlContinuous
	.Weight = xlThin
	.ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	End With
	
End Sub


Function AlanUzunluklari(XmlValue,tbl_T,i,tbl_L,LenFieldName,FlagFieldName,JusTify)
	Dim FieldLen
	Dim Fieldflag
	AlanUzunluklari="" & XmlValue
	AlanUzunluklari=Trim(AlanUzunluklari)
	AlanUzunluklari=RePlace(AlanUzunluklari,Space(2),Space(1))
	AlanUzunluklari=RePlace(AlanUzunluklari,Space(2),Space(1))
	
	if Len(AlanUzunluklari)<=0 then Exit Function
	
	FieldLen=tbl_L.GetFieldValue(LenFieldName,-1)
	Fieldflag=tbl_L.GetFieldValue(FlagFieldName,-1)
	
	if FieldLen=0 then Exit Function
	if Trim(Fieldflag)="1" then
		if JusTify="R" then
			AlanUzunluklari=Right(AlanUzunluklari,FieldLen)
		elseif JusTify="L" then
			AlanUzunluklari=Left(AlanUzunluklari,FieldLen)
		end if
		Exit Function
	end if
	
	if Len(AlanUzunluklari)>FieldLen then
		Doc.d7MsgBox i & ". Satir. Belirtilen Alan("+LenFieldName+") Uzunluðu=" & FieldLen & " Yazilmaya Çaliþilan Bilginin Uzunluðu=" & Len(AlanUzunluklari),"Freedom",MB_OK
		AlanUzunluklari=" "
	end if
End Function


Sub DeleteFile(FileName)
	dim fs
	Set fs=CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	if fs.FileExists(FileName) then
		fs.DeleteFile(FileName)
	end if
	set fs=nothing
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DUNDAR_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : IMLT00-1
Hangi Tablo için : IMLT00
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : TEKLÝF MODÜLÜ ÝÇÝNDEN TEZGAHLARI AKTARIR (EXCEL SERVERDA YÜKLÜ OLMADIÐINDAN)
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Kullanici = Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName)
	If Kullanici = "ADMIN" OR Kullanici = "FX" OR Kullanici = "AYILDIZ" Then
		Set IML00 = Doc.GetTableObject("IMLT00")
		Set TKL20TI = Doc.Select1("sdfsdf", "TEKL20TI", "EVRAKNO='1'", "", -1)
		KayitSayisi = TKL20TI.GetRecCount()
		For i = 1 to KayitSayisi
				TezgahKodu = Trim(TKL20TI.KOD(i))
				TezgahAdi = Trim(TKL20TI.STOK_AD1(i))
				Doc.Load_Voucher(TezgahKodu)
				IML00.AD = TezgahAdi
				IML00.B_KAPASITE = 20
				'IML00.B_KAPASITE_PERMPS = "20"
				IML00.B_KAPASITE6 = 20
				IML00.TAKVIMKODU = "GENEL"
				'IML00.B_KAPASITE6_PERMPS = "20"
				Doc.Save_Voucher
		Next
		MSGBOX "BÝTTÝ!!!"
	End IF
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "REVNO","CHAR",2,0,"C","",""
End Sub
'------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub EvrakAc()
	Set KTTST2CAR = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_ST2CAR")
	SatirNo=KTTST2CAR.GetCurrentRow()
	CariKodu = Trim(KTTST2CAR.ALAN1(SatirNo))
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentUI("CARI00", "")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher CariKodu
End Sub
'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Revizyon

	If Not OzelHakVarMi("REVNO") Then 
		Msgbox "Bu Özelliði Kullanma Yetkiniz Yok."
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
	Set S00 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK00")
	StokKodu = Trim(S00.KOD)
	If StokKodu = "" Then
		Msgbox "Stok Kodu Seçilmemiþ"
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "FORM_REVIZYON", SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, Par
End Sub
'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub RevizyonlariOlustur
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	If Trim(MTable.REVNO) = "" Then
		Msgbox "Revizyon No Girilmemiþ."
		Exit Sub
	Else
		RevizyonNo = Trim(MTable.REVNO)
	End If
	
	Set S00 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK00")
	StokKodu = Trim(S00.KOD)
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK00", "")
	Set S00X = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK00")
	
	YeniStokKartiKodu = StokKodu & "-" & RevizyonNo
	Set S00Kontrol = Doc.Select1("xddd", "STOK00", "KOD='"&YeniStokKartiKodu&"'", "", -1)
	If S00Kontrol.GetRecCount > 0 Then
		Msgbox "Stok Kartý Mevcut. Ýþlem Ýptal Edilmiþtir."
		Exit Sub
	Else
	
		k=doc.d7msgbox("Yeni Stok Kartý : " & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & YeniStokKartiKodu & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Koduyla Açýlacaktýr. Emin Misiniz?", "ONAY", MB_YESNO)
		If k=6 Then
			Doc.RunCommand "MT_DOC_COPY"
			Doc1.Load_Voucher YeniStokKartiKodu
			Doc1.RunCommand "MT_DOC_PASTE"
			ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
			If ret = 0 Then 
				Doc.d7MsgBox "[" & YeniStokKartiKodu & "] Kodlu Stok Kartý Oluþturuldu.", "Ýþlem Tamamlandý", MB_OK
				Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
				'Ürün Aðacý Oluþturma
				Set B01E = Doc.Select1("xxssdd", "BOMU01E", "MAMULCODE='"&StokKodu&"'", "", -1)
				If B01E.GetRecCount = 0 Then
					Msgbox "Ürün Aðacý Yok. Ýþlem Kesilmiþtir."
					Exit Sub
				Else
					EskiUrunAgaciFoyNo = Trim(B01E.EVRAKNO)
					Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("BOMU01", "")
					Set B01E = Doc1.GetTableObject("BOMU01E")
					Set B01T = Doc1.GetTableObject("BOMU01T")
					
					Doc1.Load_Voucher EskiUrunAgaciFoyNo
					Doc1.RunCommand "MT_DOC_COPY"
					Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
					
					Doc1.New_Voucher 
					YeniUrunAgaciFoyNo = Trim(B01E.EVRAKNO)
					Doc1.RunCommand "MT_DOC_PASTE"
					B01E.MAMULCODE = YeniStokKartiKodu
					ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
					If ret = 0 Then 
						Doc.d7MsgBox "[" & YeniStokKartiKodu & "] Koduna ait Ürün Aðacý Aþaðýdaki Yeni Föy No ile Oluþturuldu." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & YeniUrunAgaciFoyNo, "Ýþlem Tamamlandý", MB_OK
						Doc1.Load_Voucher EskiUrunAgaciFoyNo
						B01E.AKTIF_PASIF = " "
						Doc1.Save_Voucher
						Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
					Else
						Doc.d7MsgBox "[" & YeniStokKartiKodu & "]" & " Koduna ait Ürün Aðacý Oluþturulamadý", "Ýþlem Kesilmiþtir.", MB_OK
					End If
					Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
				End If
			Else
				Doc.d7MsgBox "[" & YeniStokKartiKodu & "]" & " Kodlu Stok Kartý Oluþturulamadý", "Ýþlem Kesilmiþtir.", MB_OK
			End If
			Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
			Doc.RUN_APP_EXIT_ONIDLE
		Else
			Exit Sub
		End If
	End If
End Sub
'---------------------------------------
'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function OzelHakVarMi(HakKodu)
	OzelHakVarMi = False
	Set U12 = Doc.Select1("de443d44t", "UDEFT12", "KOD='" & Doc.Getd7UserName() & "'&OZEL_HAK_KODU='" & HakKodu & "'", "", -1)
	if U12.GetRowCount() > 0 Then OzelHakVarMi = True
End Function

Makro Kodu : FYTMAKRO
Hangi Tablo için : PERSM0
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : GV AKTARMA ÝÞE GÝRÝÞ ÜCRET GÝRME
Kod : 
Sub IseGirisBilgileriDoldur(ByVal ExcelSheet,ByVal Satir,ByVal PersonelNo)
	Dim Tbl_PERSMBT2
	Doc.RunCommand "CMD_CALISMA_UCRET_SATIR_DEGISTIR_BASLA"
	Set Tbl_PERSMBT2=Doc.GetTableObject("PERSM0BT2")

	Dim AlanIsmi
	Dim AlanDegeri
	Dim Sutun
	For Sutun=2 To 180
		AlanIsmi=Trim(ExcelSheet.Cells(1, Sutun))
		AlanDegeri=ExcelSheet.Cells(Satir, Sutun)
		If AlanIsmi<>"" Then
			Tbl_PERSMBT2.SetFieldValue AlanIsmi,0,AlanDegeri
		End If
	Next

	Doc.RunCommand "CMD_CALISMA_UCRET_SATIR_DEGISTIR_OK"
End Sub


Sub Makro1()
	Dim myExcel
	Dim mySheet
	Set myExcel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")

	myExcel.Workbooks.Open ("F:\calýsma.XLS")
	Set mySheet = myExcel.Workbooks("calýsma.XLS").Worksheets("CALISMA")

	Dim PersonelNo

	Dim I		
	For I=2 To 180
		PersonelNo=Trim(mySheet.Cells(I, 1))
		If PersonelNo<>"" Then
			PersonelNo=Doc.LeftJustify(PersonelNo,16)
			Doc.Load_Voucher PersonelNo
			If Trim(Doc.GetLoadedVoucher())=Trim(PersonelNo) Then
				Call IseGirisBilgileriDoldur(mySheet,I,PersonelNo)
				Doc.Save_Voucher
			Else
				MsgBox(Trim(PersonelNo) & " Nolu Personel Bulunamadý")
			End If
		End If
	Next

	myExcel.Quit

	Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("f:\departman.xlsx")
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)

	Set S00 = Doc.GetTableObject("PERSM22")
	Set ddd = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	For i = 2 to 65000
		PersonelKodu = Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value
		if trim(PersonelKodu) = "" Then Exit For

		Ad = Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value

		Doc.Load_Voucher PersonelKodu
		PERSM04.ACIKLAMA = Ad
		
		Doc.Save_Voucher

	Next
	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
Dim myExcel
	Dim mySheet
	Set myExcel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")

	myExcel.Workbooks.Open ("f:\calýsma.xls")
	Set mySheet = myExcel.Workbooks("calýsma.xls").Worksheets("CALISMA")

	Dim personelkodu,departman

	Dim I,j		
	For I=2 To 600 
personelkodu=Trim(mySheet.Cells(I, 1))
departman=Trim(mySheet.Cells(I, 2))
if personelkodu<>"" then
doc.load_voucher(personelkodu)

For j=1 to PERSM0BT2.Getreccount()
if trim(PERSM0BT2.AP10(j))="1" THEN 
PERSM0BT2.HESAPSABLONU(j)=departman
end if
next

end if
Doc.Save_Voucher()
next
End sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu

Hangi Tablo için : PERSM0

Sub IseGirisBilgileriDoldur(ByVal ExcelSheet,ByVal Satir,ByVal PersonelNo)
	Dim Tbl_PERSMBT2
	Doc.RunCommand "CMD_CALISMA_UCRET_YENI_GIRIS_BASLA"
	Set Tbl_PERSMBT2=Doc.GetTableObject("PERSM0BT2")

	Dim AlanIsmi
	Dim AlanDegeri
	Dim Sutun
	For Sutun=2 To 20
		AlanIsmi=Trim(ExcelSheet.Cells(1, Sutun))
		AlanDegeri=ExcelSheet.Cells(Satir, Sutun)
		If AlanIsmi<>"" Then
			if trim(AlanIsmi) = "TARIH1" Then
				arr = split(AlanDegeri,"/")
				gun  = arr(0)
				ay = arr(1)
				yil = arr(2)
				AlanDegeri = yil &"/"& ay  &"/"& gun
			end if
			Tbl_PERSMBT2.SetFieldValue AlanIsmi,0,AlanDegeri
		End If
	Next

	Doc.RunCommand "CMD_CALISMA_UCRET_YENI_GIRIS_OK"
End Sub

Function GetEtiketTable(EtiketFormu)
	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK20" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK20_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK68" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK68_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "SAYM01" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = SAYM01_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK60_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK25" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK25_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = TRACK25_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK29" or Doc.GetDocType = "STOK29I" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK29_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK36" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK36_EtiketYarat()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK19" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK19_EtiketYarat()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK26" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = Transfer_EtiketYarat(STOK26T)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK63" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = Transfer_EtiketYarat(STOK63T)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK61" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK61_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK24" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK24_EtiketYarat()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK26" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK26_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "MMPS10" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = MMPS10_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK46" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK46_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "SFDC32" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = SFDC32_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu, Miktar)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK2A" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK2A_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "D7KIDSLB" or Doc.GetDocType = "D7KIDSPL" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = D7KIDSLB_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
		Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK21" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK21_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	End if
End Function
Function MMPS10_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")
	
	Doc1.CreateTableObject "xxx11dw=D7KIDSLB", "TMPCOPY"
	Set d7LB1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("xxx11dw")
	Set MMPS10_EtiketYarat = d7LB1

	Set M10TM = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	Set M10TI = Doc.Select2("w2f4f4f4dfwd", "MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'", "", -1)

	if M10E.STATUS <> "R" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "R statüsünde olmayan MPS 'lere etiket alýnamaz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Function
	End if

	
	M10TM.SafePack()
	For k = 1 to M10TI.GetRowCount()
'		Miktar = M10TI.R_YMAMULMIKTAR(k)
		Miktar = M10TI.R_BAKIYEYMAMULMIKTAR(k)
		KasaInfo = GetKasaInfo(M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, M10TI.R_OPERASYON(k))
		
		'  KutuIciMiktar = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10E", M10E.EVRAKNO, "KOLI_MIK",0)
		DefKutuIciMiktar = KasaInfo(0)
		if DefKutuIciMiktar = 0 Then
			OpAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01", "AD", M10TI.R_OPERASYON(k), "")
			KutuIciMiktar = InputBox("OP-" & trim(M10TI.R_SIRANO(k)) & " " & trim(OpAdi) & _
						" için Kasa Ýçi Miktar", "", DefKutuIciMiktar)
		Else
			KutuIciMiktar = DefKutuIciMiktar
		End if
		KutuIciMiktar = doc.Round(KutuIciMiktar,0)
				
		if KutuIciMiktar = 0 then KutuIciMiktar = DefKutuIciMiktar
		if KutuIciMiktar = 0 then KutuIciMiktar = Miktar
		EtiketAdedi = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(Miktar, KutuIciMiktar))
'		EtiketAdedi = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10E", M10E.EVRAKNO, "ETIK_ADT",0)
		if EtiketAdedi = 0 Then EtiketAdedi = 1
'		EtiketAdedi = InputBox("Etiket Sayýsý", "", EtiketAdedi)
		EtiketAdedi = doc.Round(EtiketAdedi,0)

		Call GetSonrakiOp(M10TI.JOBNO(k), SonrakiTezgah, SonrakiOp, SonrakiOpSiraNo)
				
		if k = M10TI.GetRowCount() Then
			UrunSayisi = M10TM.GetRowCount()
		Else
			UrunSayisi = 1
		End if

		For j = 1 to UrunSayisi
			if k = M10TI.GetRowCount() Then
'					Miktar = M10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(j)
				Miktar = M10TM.SF_BAKIYEMIKTAR(j)
				EtiketAdedi = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(Miktar, KutuIciMiktar))
				MamulKodu = M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(j)
			Else
				MamulKodu = M10TI.R_MAMULKODU
			End if
			Kalan = Miktar
			for i = 1 to EtiketAdedi
				if Kalan < KutuIciMiktar Then
					KutuMiktari = Kalan
				Else
					KutuMiktari = KutuIciMiktar
				End if			
				Kalan = Kalan - KutuMiktari
				
				BarkodNo = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
				Doc1.Load_Voucher BarkodNo
				
				d7LB.SKOD = MamulKodu
				d7LB.LOTNUMBER = LotNo
				d7LB.SERINO = trim(BarkodNo)
				d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = KutuMiktari
				d7LB.SF_UNIT = M10TM.SF_SF_UNIT(j)
				d7LB.MPSNO = M10E.EVRAKNO
				d7LB.FOKOD = M10TM.FOKOD_MAMULKODU(j)
				d7LB.MPSTM_TRNUM = M10TM.TRNUM(j)
				d7LB.JOBNO = M10TI.JOBNO(k)
				d7LB.REFTARIH1 = Doc.BuGun()
			
				CHKodu = M10TM.MUSTERIKODU(j)
				if trim(CHKodu) = "" Then
					CHKodu = GetMusteriCHKodu(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(j))
				End if
				d7LB.CH_MUSTERI = CHKodu
				d7LB.FORMKODU = EtiketFormu
				d7LB.STATU = ""
				d7LB.APPKODU = "MMPS10"
				d7LB.EVRAKTYPE = "UP"
				d7LB.EVRAKNO = M10E.EVRAKNO
				d7LB.REFTEXT01 = SonrakiOp
				d7LB.REFTEXT02 = SonrakiOpSiraNo
				d7LB.REFTEXT501 = SonrakiTezgah
				d7LB.REFTEXT04 = KasaInfo(1)
				Doc1.Save_Voucher
				Set d7LB2 = Doc1.Select2("___D7KIDSLB1", "D7KIDSLB", "", "", -1)
				d7LB1.AppendTable(d7LB2)
			Next
		Next
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	Set MMPS10_EtiketYarat = d7LB1
End Function
Function STOK26_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
End Function
Function Palet_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	if Doc.IsBlank(D7KIDSPL) Then Set D7KIDSPL = Doc.GetTableObject("D7KIDSPL")
	QStr = "SELECT SF_MIKTAR FROM STOK01"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE 1=0 "
	For i = 1 to D7KIDSPL.GetRowCount()
		QStr = QStr & " or (KOD='" & D7KIDSPL.SKOD(i) & "' AND SERINO='" & D7KIDSPL.SERINO(i) & "')"
	Next
	Set S01 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("erg35fgdfg", QStr)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = D7KIDSLB.GetRowCount() Then
		S01.GroupBy("KOD+SERINO")
		if S01.GetRowCount() = D7KIDSLB.GetRowCount() Then
			Miktar = S01.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR")
		Else
			Miktar = 0
		End if
	End if
	Set Palet_EtiketYarat = Doc.Select2G("sdf34rgsdfg", "D7KIDSPL", "", "", "KOD", -1)
	Palet_EtiketYarat.SF_MIKTAR = Miktar
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD1","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD2","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD3","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD4","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TUMRESIMNODOLU", "CHAR", 1, 0, "L", "", ""
	MTable.Addfield2 "RESIMNOAYNI", "CHAR", 1, 0, "L", "", ""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SATINALINAN", "CHAR", 1, 0, "L", "", ""
	MTable.Addfield2 "RESIMNO", "CHAR", 24, 0, "L", "", ""
	
	Dim MTable2
	Set MTable2=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLE2=STACK","IO")
	MTable2.Addfield2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_1","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_2","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_3","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_4","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_5","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_6","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_7","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_8","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_9","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_10","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "RESIMNO","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	
	'Set S00 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK00")
	'MSGBOX 	S00.KOD

	MTable.SATINALINAN = "H"
	MTable.TUMRESIMNODOLU = "H"
	MTable.RESIMNOAYNI = "H"

Sub Suz()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE2")
	MTable2.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTable2.Empty
	
	If MTable.SATINALINAN = "H" AND MTable.TUMRESIMNODOLU = "H" AND MTable.RESIMNOAYNI = "H" AND Trim(MTable.KOD) = "" AND Trim(MTable.AD1) = "" AND Trim(MTable.AD2) = "" AND Trim(MTable.RESIMNO) = "" Then
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
	If MTable.SATINALINAN = "E" Then 
		Kriter1 = "KART_TIPI = 'S'"
	Else
		Kriter1 = "KART_TIPI <> ' '"
	End If
	
	If MTable.TUMRESIMNODOLU = "E" Then 
		Kriter2 = "TSENO <> ' '"
	Else
		Kriter2 = "TSENO <> '56564664546GTGDFGG4T45THRH'"
	End If

	If MTable.RESIMNOAYNI = "E" Then 
		MTable.TUMRESIMNODOLU = "H"
		MTable.SATINALINAN = "H"
		MTable.KOD = ""
		MTable.AD1 = ""
		MTable.AD2 = ""
		MTable.AD3 = ""
		MTable.AD4 = ""
		MTable.RESIMNO = ""
		
			QStr = "SELECT *"  & _
					" FROM STOK00"  & _
					" WHERE TSENO  <> ' ' AND " & "TSENO IN (SELECT TSENO FROM STOK00 GROUP BY TSENO HAVING COUNT(*) > 1) ORDER BY TSENO DESC"
	Else
		MTable.RESIMNOAYNI = ""
		QStr = "SELECT *"  & _
					" FROM STOK00"  & _
					" WHERE 1=1 AND " & Kriter1 & "AND " & Kriter2
					
		If Trim(MTable.KOD) <> "" Then
			QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.KOD) LIKE '%" & UCase(Trim(MTable.KOD)) & "%'"
		End If
		
		If Trim(MTable.AD1) <> "" Then
			QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(Trim(MTable.AD1)) & "%'"
		End If

		If Trim(MTable.AD2) <> "" Then
			QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(Trim(MTable.AD2)) & "%'"
		End If

		If Trim(MTable.AD3) <> "" Then
			QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(Trim(MTable.AD3)) & "%'"
		End If
		
		If Trim(MTable.AD4) <> "" Then
			QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(Trim(MTable.AD4)) & "%'"
		End If
		
		If Trim(MTable.RESIMNO) <> "" Then
			QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.TSENO) LIKE '%" & UCase(Trim(MTable.RESIMNO)) & "%'"
			QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY TSENO DESC"
		End If
	End If
 
	Doc.RunSQLQuery "XXXX_1", QStr
	Set S00 = Doc.GetTableobject("XXXX_1")
	For i = 1 to S00.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Listeye Ekleniyor : " & i & " / " & S00.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTable2.AddRow()
		MTable2.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTable2.KOD = S00.KOD(i)
		MTable2.AD = S00.AD(i)
		MTable2.GK_1 = S00.GK_1(i)
		MTable2.GK_2 = S00.GK_2(i)
		MTable2.GK_3 = S00.GK_3(i)
		MTable2.GK_4 = S00.GK_4(i)
		MTable2.GK_5 = S00.GK_5(i)
		MTable2.GK_6 = S00.GK_6(i)
		MTable2.GK_7 = S00.GK_7(i)
		MTable2.GK_8 = S00.GK_8(i)
		MTable2.GK_9 = S00.GK_9(i)
		MTable2.GK_10 = S00.GK_10(i)
		MTable2.RESIMNO= S00.TSENO(i)
	Next
	MTable2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	MTable2.SetCurrentRow(1)
End Sub
'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Sec()
	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE2")
	'Satir = Mtable2.GetCurrentRow
	'StokKodu = Trim(MTable2.KOD(Satir))
	StokKodu = MTable2.KOD
	If StokKodu = "" Then Exit Sub
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
	Set T1 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK00")
	T1.KOD = StokKodu
End Sub

Sub Sifirla
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE2")
	MTable.Empty
	MTable2.Empty
	MTable.SATINALINAN = "H"
	MTable.TUMRESIMNODOLU = "H"
	MTable.RESIMNOAYNI = "H"
End Sub

	GROUPBOX TEXT="" AUTOSIZE=N LEFT=0 TOP=0p WIDTH=950P HEIGHT=450P BORDER=RAISED TXTCOL=RGB_000_089_132 BCKCOL=RGB_000_000_000

			LTEXT LEFT=1 TOP=E+0p T="     (Tüm Stok Kodlarý için Kutu Boþ Olmalý.)"

		ENDGROUPBOX

Sub Suz()

	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE2")
	MTable2.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTable2.Empty
	
	If MTable.SATINALINAN = "H" AND MTable.TUMRESIMNODOLU = "H" AND MTable.RESIMNOAYNI = "H" AND Trim(MTable.KOD) = "" AND Trim(MTable.AD1) = "" AND Trim(MTable.AD2) = "" AND Trim(MTable.RESIMNO) = "" Then
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
	If MTable.SATINALINAN = "E" Then 
		Kriter1 = "KART_TIPI = 'S'"
	Else
		Kriter1 = "KART_TIPI <> ' '"
	End If
	
	If MTable.TUMRESIMNODOLU = "E" Then 
		Kriter2 = "SUPPLIERCODE <> ' '"
	Else
		Kriter2 = "SUPPLIERCODE <> '56564664546GTGDFGG4T45THRH'"
	End If

		MTable.AD1 = ""

		MTable.AD3 = ""

			QStr = "SELECT *"  & _
					" FROM STOK00"  & _
					" WHERE SUPPLIERCODE  <> ' ' AND " & "SUPPLIERCODE IN (SELECT SUPPLIERCODE FROM STOK00 GROUP BY SUPPLIERCODE HAVING COUNT(*) > 1) ORDER BY SUPPLIERCODE DESC"
	Else
		MTable.RESIMNOAYNI = ""
		QStr = "SELECT *"  & _
					" FROM STOK00"  & _
					" WHERE 1=1 AND " & Kriter1 & "AND " & Kriter2
					
		If Trim(MTable.KOD) <> "" Then
			QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.KOD) LIKE '%" & UCase(Trim(MTable.KOD)) & "%'"
		End If

		End If

		If Trim(MTable.AD2) <> "" Then
			QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(Trim(MTable.AD2)) & "%'"
		End If

		End If

			QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(Trim(MTable.AD4)) & "%'"
		End If

			QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.SUPPLIERCODE) LIKE '%" & UCase(Trim(MTable.RESIMNO)) & "%'"
			QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY SUPPLIERCODE DESC"
		End If
	End If
 
	Doc.RunSQLQuery "XXXX_1", QStr
	Set S00 = Doc.GetTableobject("XXXX_1")
	For i = 1 to S00.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Listeye Ekleniyor : " & i & " / " & S00.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTable2.AddRow()
		MTable2.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTable2.KOD = S00.KOD(i)
		MTable2.AD = S00.AD(i)
		MTable2.GK_1 = S00.GK_1(i)
		MTable2.GK_2 = S00.GK_2(i)
		MTable2.GK_3 = S00.GK_3(i)
		MTable2.GK_4 = S00.GK_4(i)
		MTable2.GK_5 = S00.GK_5(i)
		MTable2.GK_6 = S00.GK_6(i)
		MTable2.GK_7 = S00.GK_7(i)
		MTable2.GK_8 = S00.GK_8(i)
		MTable2.GK_9 = S00.GK_9(i)
		MTable2.GK_10 = S00.GK_10(i)
		MTable2.RESIMNO= S00.SUPPLIERCODE(i)
	Next
	MTable2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	MTable2.SetCurrentRow(1)
End Sub

Sub Sec()
	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE2")
	'Satir = Mtable2.GetCurrentRow
	'StokKodu = Trim(MTable2.KOD(Satir))
	StokKodu = MTable2.KOD
	If StokKodu = "" Then Exit Sub
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
	Set T1 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK74T")
	T1.KOD = StokKodu
End Sub

Sub Sifirla
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	MTable.Empty
	MTable2.Empty
	MTable.SATINALINAN = "H"
	MTable.TUMRESIMNODOLU = "H"
	MTable.RESIMNOAYNI = "H"
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set xmlDoc = CreateObject("Microsoft.XMLDOM")
	xmlDoc.async = "false"
	xmlDoc.Load ("c:\inv.xml")
	Set Irs = xmlDoc.selectNodes("/INV_DATA/INV_DATA_DETAIL/NO_DOCUMENT")
	For i = 0 To (Irs.Length - 1)
		Doc.StatusBarMessage i & " / " & Irs.Length
		IrsNo = Irs.Item(i).Text
		SiraNo = GetSiraNo(IrsNo)
		if SiraNo <> 0 Then
			STOK60E.SetCurrentRow(SiraNo)
			STOK60E.SELECTED(SiraNo) = "E"
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function GetSiraNo(IrsaliyeNo)
	GetSiraNo = 0
	for i = 1 to STOK60E.GetRowCount()
		if InStr(1,STOK60E.EVRAKNO(i), trim(IrsaliyeNo)) > 0 Then
			GetSiraNo = i
			Exit Function
		End if
	Next
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DUNDAR_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : KARSILASTI
Hangi Tablo için : STOK40
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : 
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Call GerceklesenOngoruKarsilastir()
End Sub
Sub GerceklesenOngoruKarsilastir()
	Set fff = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	Set S40T = Doc.Select2("Sedrt44", "STOK40T", "", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to S40T.GetRowCount()
		S40T.REV_RTESTARIH(i) = Left(S40T.REV_RTESTARIH(i), 8) & "01"
	Next
	S40T.GroupBy("KOD+REV_RTESTARIH")
	For i = 1 to S40T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Öngörü/Gerçekleþen Kontrolü Yapýlýyor : " & i & "/" & S40T.GetRowCount()
		OngoruMiktari = GetOngoruMiktari(S40T.KOD(i), STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU, S40T.REV_RTESTARIH(i))
		GerceklesenMiktar = S40T.SF_MIKTAR(i) + GetOncekiGerceklesenMiktar(S40T.KOD(i), STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU, S40T.REV_RTESTARIH(i), STOK40E.EVRAKNO)
		
		if GerceklesenMiktar > OngoruMiktari Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Kodu : " & trim(S40T.KOD(i)) & " Öngörü Miktarý : " & OngoruMiktari & _
				" Gerçekleþen Sipariþ : " & GerceklesenMiktar, "", MB_OK
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function GetOngoruMiktari(StokKodu, CHKodu, TeslimTarihi)
	GetOngoruMiktari = 0
'	OngoruSipTarihi = Doc.StrDate_Increment(TeslimTarihi, -60)
	OngoruSipTarihi = GetNAyOnce(TeslimTarihi, 2)
	Set d7Date = Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	d7Date.SetDate OngoruSipTarihi
	d7Date.AyinIlkGununeGit
	OngoruBasi = d7Date.GetRevDate
	d7Date.AyinSonGununeGit
	OngoruSonu = d7Date.GetRevDate

	d7Date.SetDate TeslimTarihi
	d7Date.AyinIlkGununeGit
	TeslimTarihiBasi = d7Date.GetRevDate
	d7Date.AyinSonGununeGit
	TeslimTarihiSonu = d7Date.GetRevDate

	QStr = "KOD='" & StokKodu & "'&MUSTERIKODU='" & CHKodu & "'&TARIH>='" & OngoruBasi & "'&TARIH<='" & OngoruSonu & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&REV_RTESTARIH>='" & TeslimTarihiBasi & "'&REV_RTESTARIH<='" & TeslimTarihiSonu & "'"
	Set OSP1L = Doc.Select1("dd34r4r4r4", "OTOSP1L", QStr, "TARIH+EVRAKNO", -1)
	if OSP1L.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	OSP1L.GroupBy("TARIH")
	For i = 1 to OSP1L.GetRowCount()
		if i > 1 and OSP1L.TARIH(i) <> EskiTarih Then Exit For

		GetOngoruMiktari = GetOngoruMiktari + OSP1L.SF_MIKTAR(i)

		EskiTarih = OSP1L.TARIH(i)
	Next
End Function
Function GetOncekiGerceklesenMiktar(StokKodu, CHKodu, TeslimTarihi, SiparisNo)
	GetOncekiGerceklesenMiktar = 0
	Set d7Date = Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	d7Date.SetDate TeslimTarihi
	d7Date.AyinIlkGununeGit
	TeslimTarihiBasi = d7Date.GetRevDate
	d7Date.AyinSonGununeGit
	TeslimTarihiSonu = d7Date.GetRevDate

	QStr = "KOD='" & StokKodu & "'&MUSTERIKODU='" & CHKodu & "'&REV_RTESTARIH>='" & TeslimTarihiBasi & _
							"'&REV_RTESTARIH<='" & TeslimTarihiSonu & "'&EVRAKNO<>'" & SiparisNo & "'"
	Set S40T = Doc.Select1("dd34r4r4r4", "STOK40T", QStr, "", -1)
	if S40T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		S40T.GroupBy("KOD")
		GetOncekiGerceklesenMiktar = S40T.SF_MIKTAR
	End if
End Function
Function GetNAyOnce(Tarih, OnceAy)
	Yil = Doc.StrDate_Year(Tarih)
	Ay = Doc.StrDate_Month(Tarih)
	Gun = Doc.StrDate_Day(Tarih)

	YeniAy = Ay - OnceAy
	if YeniAy <= 0 Then
		YeniAy = YeniAy + 12
		Yil = Yil - 1
	End if
	GetNAyOnce = Yil & "/" & Right("0" & YeniAy, 2) & "/" & Right("0" & Gun,2)
End Function

Sub Makro1
	Set T_STOK29E = Doc.CreateRowSetObject("XXX=STOK29E", "REPORT") 
	Set Kriterler = Doc.CreateSObject("Query")
	Kriterler.UseTable "STOK29E"

	Next

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DUNDAR_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : KODARA2

Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için :

Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "KOD_ARANACAKDEYIM","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTable.KOD_ARANACAKDEYIM=""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD_ARANACAKDEYIM","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.AD_ARANACAKDEYIM=""

	GFLS_Miktar = Doc.Customp_Gets("GFLS_MIK")
	GFLSArr = Split(trim(GFLS_Miktar), ".")
	if uBound(GFLSArr) = 1 Then
		GFLSInt = Doc.CDbl(GFLSArr(0))
		GFLSDec = Doc.CDbl(GFLSArr(1))
	Else
		GFLSInt = 12
		GFLSDec = 4
	End if
	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=STACK","IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "IUNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE",GFLSInt,GFLSDec,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "REZERVE_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE",GFLSInt,GFLSDec,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SERBEST_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE",GFLSInt,GFLSDec,"R","",""
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub

Sub GO_KOD()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	If Doc.IsBlank(MTable.KOD_ARANACAKDEYIM) Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Aranýlacak Deyim Giriniz","",-1
		Exit Sub
	End If

	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")	
	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT.Empty
	Dim Tbl
	Set Tbl=Doc.Select1("__MAK111","STOK00","KOD BENZER '" & Trim(MTable.KOD_ARANACAKDEYIM) & "'&AP10='1'","KOD",-1)

	Dim I 
	For I=1 to Tbl.GetRowCount()
		MTableT.AddRow
		MTableT.SetCurrentRow(I)
		StokKodu = Tbl.KOD(I)
		ToplamMiktar = Doc.Stok00c_SonMik(StokKodu, "")
		RezerveMiktar = Doc.Stok00c_SonRezerve(StokKodu,"","")
		SerbestMiktar = ToplamMiktar - RezerveMiktar
		MTableT.KOD = StokKodu
		MTableT.AD=Tbl.AD(I)
		MTableT.TOPLAM_MIKTAR = ToplamMiktar
		MTableT.REZERVE_MIKTAR = RezerveMiktar
		MTableT.SERBEST_MIKTAR = SerbestMiktar
	Next
	
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	Doc.DeleteTableObject "__MAK111"

End Sub
Sub GO_AD()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	If Doc.IsBlank(MTable.AD_ARANACAKDEYIM) Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Aranýlacak Deyim Giriniz","",-1
		Exit Sub
	End If

	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")	
	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT.Empty
	Dim Tbl
	Set Tbl=Doc.Select1("__MAK111","STOK00","AD BENZER '" & Trim(MTable.AD_ARANACAKDEYIM) & "'","KOD",-1)

	Dim I 
	For I=1 to Tbl.GetRowCount()
		MTableT.AddRow
		MTableT.SetCurrentRow(I)
		MTableT.KOD=Tbl.KOD(I)
		MTableT.AD=Tbl.AD(I)
	Next
	
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	Doc.DeleteTableObject "__MAK111"
End Sub
Sub GO_SEC()
	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")	
	Secilensatir = MTableT.GetCurrentRow()
	StokKodu = MTableT.KOD(Secilensatir)
	Set STOK00 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK00")
	STOK00.KOD = StokKodu
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DUNDAR_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : STOK-001
Hangi Tablo için : STOK001
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : TOPLU STOK GÜNCELLEMEDE <HER ZAMAN ÜSTÜNE YAZ> SEÇÝLÝ HALE GETÝR
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Kullanici = Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName)
	If Kullanici = "ADMIN" OR Kullanici = "AYILDIZ" Then
		Set ALN = Doc.GetTableObject("ALANLAR")
		KayitSayisi = ALN.GetRecCount()
		For i = 1 to KayitSayisi
			ALN.ALAN_OPT1(i) = "1"
		Next
	End If
End Sub

Sub Makro1()

	Kullanici = Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName)
	If Kullanici = "MAKDOGAN" Then
		KayitSayisi = LISTET.GetRecCount
		Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK00", "")
			
		For i=1 to KayitSayisi
			EvrakNo=LISTET.KOD(i)
			If Trim(EvrakNo)<>"" Then
				Doc1.Load_Voucher(EvrakNo)
				Doc1.Delete_Voucher()			
			End If
		Next
	End If
End Sub

Sub OPERATORADI
	Set KTTIM03 = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_IM03")
	OpKodu = KTTIM03.OPKOD
	OpAdi = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERS00", "AD", OpKodu, ""))
	KTTIM03.OPAD = OpAdi
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DUNDAR_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : KTA
Hangi Tablo için : CARI001
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Toplu KTA giriþi
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "ARANACAKDEYIM","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.ARANACAKDEYIM=""

	AnaTabloStr = "LISTET"
	Set AnaTablo = Doc.GetTableObject("LISTET")
	AnaTablo.SafePack()
	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=STACK","IO")

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	Call AddAllFields(MTableT)
	Call AddUDEFFields(MTableT)

	Call AlanlariDoldur(AnaTablo, MTableT)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	EkranFormu = GetEkranFormu(AnaTabloStr, AnaTablo)
'	Call MsgBoxToTxt("", EkranFormu)
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",EkranFormu, Par
End Sub
Function GetEkranFormu(AnaTabloStr, AnaTablo)
'	EnBoy = GetScreenWidthAndHeight()
'	En = EnBoy(0)
'	Boy = EnBoy(1)
'	if En = 0 or Boy = 0 Then
		En = 1000
		Boy = 690
'	Else
'		En = En - 24
'		Boy = Boy - 60
'	End if 
	GridEn = En - 25
	GridBoy = Boy - 90

	FormStr = "FORM MAKROFORMU1 HEIGHT=" & Boy & "P WIDTH=" & En & "P SCROLLBARS=NO CAPTIONTYPE=SYS" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SET CAPTION= " & Chr(34) & "Sipariþ Seviyeli MPS Yaratma" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "MENUDEF" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   POPUP TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Dosya" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   MENUITEM TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Ýþlem Yapmadan Çý&kýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL KEY=ESCAPE" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   ENDPOPUP" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDMENUDEF" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "LOCATE TOP=10p" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "//LTEXT LEFT=1 TOP=+0 T='Ünvanda Geçen Deyim : '" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "//EDITTEXT LEFT=E+10 TOP=+0 D=MAKROTABLE.ARANACAKDEYIM" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "GRID LEFT=1 TOP=+0 HEIGHT=" & GridBoy & "p WIDTH=" & GridEn & "p TABLE=MAKROTABLET HEADERROWHEIGHT=35P" & vbCrLf

	MainTableStr = GetMainTableStr()
	FormStr = FormStr & MainTableStr & vbCrLf

	UDFStr = GetUDFStr()
	FormStr = FormStr & UDFStr & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDGRID" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=3 TOP=E+7p WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "KTA 'larý Kaydet" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','KTA_Yaz')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
'	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=30 TOP=+0 WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Açýlacak MPS 'leri Hesapla" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','MPSHesapla')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
'	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=E+2 TOP=+0 WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "MPS 'leri Yarat" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','MPSOlustur')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=E+2 TOP=+0 WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Çýkýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDFORM" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = Replace(FormStr, "@@@BUMAKRO@@@", SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME)

	GetEkranFormu = FormStr
End Function
Function GetUDFStr()
	UDFStr = ""
	Set U10T = Doc.Select1("dd333", "UFLD10T", "EVRAKNO='CARI00'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to U10T.GetRowCount()
		AlanAdi = "UDEFF__" & trim(U10T.VARCODE(i))
		AlanIndex = U10T.VARINDEX(i)
		if trim(AlanIndex) <> "" Then
			AlanAdi = AlanAdi & "_" & trim(AlanIndex)
		End if
		Aciklama = trim(U10T.VARASPNAME(i))
		AlanTuru = trim(U10T.VARTYPE(i))
		Grupkodu = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT3(i))
		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		Else
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "EDITTEXT D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		End if
	Next
	GetUDFStr = UDFStr
End Function
Sub AddUDEFFields(MTableT)
	Set U10T = Doc.Select1("dd333", "UFLD10T", "EVRAKNO='CARI00'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to U10T.GetRowCount()
		AlanAdi = "UDEFF__" & trim(U10T.VARCODE(i))
		AlanIndex = U10T.VARINDEX(i)
		if trim(AlanIndex) <> "" Then
			AlanAdi = AlanAdi & "_" & trim(AlanIndex)
		End if
		Aciklama = trim(U10T.VARASPNAME(i))
		AlanTuru = trim(U10T.VARTYPE(i))
		Grupkodu = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT3(i))
		Tablo = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT4(i))
		Uzunluk = U10T.VARLEN(i)
		Ondalik = U10T.VARSIG(i)

		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DATE",10,0,"L","",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",Grupkodu
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Tablo) <> "" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","F2=DO_BRW('" & Tablo & "')",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "N" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DOUBLE",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"R","",""
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub AddAllFields(MTableT)
	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", "LISTET")
	For i = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		if AlanAdi = "KOD" or AlanAdi = "AD" Then
			Aciklama = Grid(i,2)
			AlanTuru = Grid(i,4)
			Grupkodu = ""
			Tablo = ""
			Uzunluk = Grid(i,5)
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			if AlanTuru = "D" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DATE",10,0,"L","",""
			Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",Grupkodu
			Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Tablo) <> "" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","F2=DO_BRW('" & Tablo & "')",""
			Elseif AlanTuru = "C" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",""
			Elseif AlanTuru = "N" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DOUBLE",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"R","",""
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function GetMainTableStr()
	MainTableStr = ""
	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", "LISTET")
	For i = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		if AlanAdi = "KOD" or AlanAdi = "AD" Then
			Aciklama = Replace(Grid(i,2), "\n", " ")
			Aciklama = Replace(Aciklama, chr(13), " ")
			Aciklama = Replace(Aciklama, chr(10), " ")
			AlanTuru = Grid(i,4)
			Grupkodu = ""
			Tablo = ""
			if AlanTuru = "D" Then
				MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
			Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
				MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
			Else
				MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "EDITTEXT D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
			End if
		End if
	Next
	GetMainTableStr = MainTableStr
End Function
Sub MsgBoxToTxt(MesajBaslik, Mesaj)
	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	tmpFolder = fso.GetSpecialFolder(2)
	dosyaAdi = tmpFolder & "\Hata.txt"
	Set HataFile = Fso.OpenTextFile(dosyaAdi, 2, True)
	HataFile.WriteLine(MesajBaslik & " : " & vbcrlf & Mesaj)
	HataFile.Close
	Set wshShell = CreateObject("WSCript.shell")
	wshshell.run "notepad " & dosyaAdi, 1, True
	set wshshell = nothing
End Sub
Sub AlanlariDoldur(AnaTablo, MainTableT)
	MainTableT.Empty
	Alanlar1 = MainTableT.GetFieldNames("")
	For i = 1 to AnaTablo.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MainTableT.AddRow()
		For each Alan in Alanlar1
			if InStr(1,Alan, "UDEFF__") > 0 or Alan = "KOD" or Alan = "AD" Then
				if InStr(1,Alan, "UDEFF__") = 0 Then
					AlanDegeri = LISTET.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
				Else
					AlanAdiIndex = GetAlanAdiIndex(Mid(Alan, 8), "CARI00")
					KTA_Alan = AlanAdiIndex(0)
					KTA_Index = AlanAdiIndex(1)

					AlanDegeri = Doc.GetUDFValue("CARI00", LISTET.KOD(i), KTA_Alan, KTA_Index)
				End if
				MainTableT.SetFieldValue Alan, satirNo, AlanDegeri
			End if
		Next
	Next
End Sub
Sub KTA_Yaz()
	Doc.Setsaveable true
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set AnaTablo = Doc.GetTableObject("LISTET")
	Alanlar = MTableT.GetFieldNames("")
	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001", "CARI00", MTableT.KOD(i))
		Set T_U1 = Doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")   
'msgbox 111
		For each Alan in Alanlar
			if InStr(1,Alan, "UDEFF__") > 0 Then
				KTA_Alan = Mid(Alan, 8)
				AlanDegeri = MTableT.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
				K = T_U1.SetFieldValue(KTA_Alan,-1,AlanDegeri)
				T_U1.Exec "SAVE" 
			End if
		Next 
'msgbox 112
	Next
	Doc.Setsaveable false
End Sub
Function GetAlanAdiIndex(AlanAdi, KTA_Tablo)
	AlanAdi = trim(AlanAdi)
	Dim AlanArr(1)
	AlanArr(0) = AlanAdi
	AlanArr(1) = 0

	GetAlanAdiIndex = AlanArr
	tire = Instr(1,AlanAdi, "_")
	If tire = 0 Then Exit Function

	If Right(AlanAdi,1) =  "_" Then Exit Function
	If Right(AlanAdi,1) < "0" or Right(AlanAdi,1) > "9" Then Exit Function

	Set U10T = Doc.Select1("dd3333", "UFLD10T", "EVRAKNO='CARI00'&VARCODE='" & AlanAdi & "'", "", -1)
	if U10T.GetRowCount() > 0 and Len(AlanAdi) <= Len(U10T.VARCODE) Then Exit Function

	Uzunluk = Len(AlanAdi)
	if Uzunluk < 3 Then Exit Function
	For i = Uzunluk-1 to Uzunluk-3 step -1
		chr1 = Mid(AlanAdi, i, 1)
		if chr1 = "_" Then
			AlanArr(0) = Left(AlanAdi, i-1)
			AlanArr(1) = Doc.CDbl(Mid(AlanAdi, i+1))
		End if
	Next
	GetAlanAdiIndex = AlanArr
End Function
Function GetScreenWidthAndHeight()
	Dim ScrArr(1)
	ScrArr(0) = 0
	ScrArr(1) = 0

	Set IE = CreateObject("InternetExplorer.Application")
	IE.Visible = False
	IE.Navigate ("about:blank")
	Set Scr = IE.Document.parentWindow.screen
	Width = Scr.Width
	Height = Scr.Height
	AvailableWidth = Scr.availwidth
	AvailableHeight = Scr.availheight
	BitsPerPixel = Scr.colordepth

	ScrArr(0) = AvailableWidth
	ScrArr(1) = AvailableHeight

	Set Scr = Nothing
	IE.Quit
	Set IE = Nothing
	GetScreenWidthAndHeight = ScrArr
End Function
