Option Explicit

Dim FileName
Dim SheetName
Dim FileName2
Dim SheetName2

const xlAutomatic=-4105
const xlCenter=-4108
const xlLeft=-4131
const xlRight=-4152
const xlContext=-5002
const xlContinuous=1
const xlThin=2

const xlEdgeBottom=9
const xlEdgeLeft=7
const xlEdgeRight=10
const xlEdgeTop=8

FileName="C:\Listeler\Ihrac Kayitli Satis Faturalari\Ihrac Kayitli Satis Fatura Listesi.xls"
SheetName="Ihrac Kayitli Satis Faturalari"

Sub Makro1()
	
	Dim i
	Dim Excel
	Dim Wb
	Dim Sh
	
	Dim cpt
	cpt=Doc.Customp_Gets("EBYNDOSD")
	cpt=Trim(cpt)
	if cpt<>"" then
		FileName=RePlace(FileName,"C:\Listeler\",cpt)
		if CreateIfNotExistDir(cpt+"Ihrac Kayitli Satis Faturalari") then
			Exit Sub
		end if
	else
		if CreateIfNotExistDir("C:\Listeler") then
			Exit Sub
		end if
		if CreateIfNotExistDir("C:\Listeler\Ihrac Kayitli Satis Faturalari") then
			Exit Sub
		end if
	end if
	
	Doc.StatusbarMessage "Excel Açiliyor"
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb = Excel.Workbooks.Add
	Set Sh = Wb.Sheets(1)
	Sh.Name = SheetName
	
	Dim DeleteSheets()
	ReDim DeleteSheets(Wb.Sheets.Count)
	
	For i = 1 To Wb.Sheets.Count
		If Trim(Wb.Sheets(i).Name) <> SheetName Then
			DeleteSheets(i) = Wb.Sheets(i).Name
		End If
	Next
	
	For i = 1 To UBound(DeleteSheets)
		If Trim(DeleteSheets(i)) <> "" Then
			Wb.Sheets(DeleteSheets(i)).Delete
		End If
	Next
	
	Call SetHeader(Sh)
	Call SetData(Sh)
	
	Call DeleteFile(FileName)
	
	Excel.ActiveWorkbook.SaveAs FileName
	
	Excel.Quit
	Set Excel=nothing
	
	Doc.d7MsgBox "Oluþturulan Dosya-> "+FileName,"Freedom",MB_OK
	
	Dim tbl_EBEYNM2E
	Set tbl_EBEYNM2E=Doc.GetTableObject("EBEYNM2E")
	tbl_EBEYNM2E.XLSFILENAME1=FileName
	
End Sub
Sub SetHeader(Sh)
	
	Call WriteCel(Sh,2, 8, 18, 3.71,  "Arial", 14, True,False,xlCenter,"","ÝHRAÇ KAYITLI SATIÞ FATURASI LiSTESÝ")
	
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 2, 76.75, 3.71,  "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Sýra No")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 3, 76.75, 11.86, "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Ýhraç Kayýtlý Satýþ Faturasýnýn Tarihi")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 4, 76.75, 7.86,  "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Ýhraç Kayýtlý Satýþ Faturasýnýn Serisi")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 5, 76.75, 10.29, "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Ýhraç Kayýtlý Satýþ Faturasýnýn Sira No'su")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 6, 76.75, 20, "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Alýcýnýn Adý-Soyadý / Ünvaný")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 7, 76.75, 14,  "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Alýcýnýn Vergi Kimlik Numarasý / TC Kimlik Numarasý")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 8, 76.75, 16.29, "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Malýn Cinsi")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 9, 76.75, 11.14, "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Malýn Miktarý")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4,10, 76.75, 12,  "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Malýn KDV Hariç Tutarý")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4,11, 76.75, 8.43,  "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Malýn KDV'si")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4,12, 76.75, 15,  "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Ýhracatçý Tarafýndan Yurt Dýþýna Düzenlenen Satýþ Faturasýnýn Tarihi(GÇB Üzerindeki Bilgiler)")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4,13, 76.75, 13.57, "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","GÇB Tescil No")
End Sub

Sub SetData(Sh)
	Dim i
	Dim tbl_EBEYNM21
	Dim FT_IVD_KH_TOPLAM
	Dim FT_KDV_TUTARI
	Dim Qstr,S70T
	Dim Donembasi,donemsonu
	' Set tbl_EBEYNM21=Doc.GetTableObject("EBEYNM21")
	
	Donembasi = "01"& "/"& EBEYNM2E.IDB_AY &"/"& EBEYNM2E.IDB_YIL
	Donembasi = Doc.AyinilkGunu(Donembasi)
	Donemsonu = Doc.AyinSonGunu(Donembasi)
	
	Set tbl_EBEYNM21 = Doc.Select1G("ds1224","STOK70T","NITELIK='XK' & TARIH >= '"& Donembasi  &"' & TARIH <= '"& Donemsonu &"'","","EVRAKNO",-1)
	
	'    QStr = "SELECT STOK70T.* FROM STOK70T"
	'    QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK70E ON STOK70T.EVRAKNO=STOK70E.EVRAKNO"
	'    QStr = QStr & " WHERE STOK70E.NITELIK='XK'"
	'    QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY EVRAKNO"
	
	'    Set S70T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("vedrg35h35hgtfhg", QStr)
	
	Dim tbl_EBEYNM2A,tbl_EBYNM222
	Set tbl_EBEYNM2A=Doc.GetTableObject("EBEYNM2A")
	Dim ftserino,ftsirano,chkodu, chkoduadi,vergino,KDVharic,KDV,gcbtarih,gcbno,KDVharic_TOPLAM, KDV_TUTARI
	Dim miktar,zz,sira_no,tanim,gcb1,gcb2,zzz,faturagosterimsayisi
	
	sira_no = 0
	
	for i=1 to tbl_EBEYNM21.GetRecCount()
		tbl_EBEYNM21.SetCurrentRow i
		
		faturagosterimsayisi = 1
		gcb1 = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK70E",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"GCBN",0)
		gcb2 = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK70E",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"GCBNA",0)
		
		if trim(gcb1) = "" then
			faturagosterimsayisi = 1
		Elseif trim(gcb1) <> "" and trim(gcb2) = "" then
			faturagosterimsayisi = 2
		Elseif trim(gcb2) <> "" then
			faturagosterimsayisi = 3
		End if
		
		for zzz=1 to faturagosterimsayisi
			
			'  sira_no = i
			sira_no = sira_no + 1
			
			ftserino = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","OR_FATURASERINO",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")
			ftsirano = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","OR_FATURASIRANO",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")
			chkodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","CH_KODU",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")
			chkoduadi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","AD",chkodu,"")
			vergino = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","VERGINO",chkodu,"")
			miktar=""
			KDVharic = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","TL_IVD_KH_TOPLAM",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")
			KDV = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","TL_KDVLERTOPLAM",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")

			if zzz=1 then
				gcbtarih = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","GCBTARIHI",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")
				gcbtarih = Doc.StrToNorDate(gcbtarih)
				gcbno = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","GCBNO",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")
			elseif zzz=2 then
				gcbno = gcb1
				gcbtarih = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK70E",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"GCBT",0)
			elseif zzz=3 then
				gcbno = gcb2
				gcbtarih = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK70E",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"GCBTA",0)
			End if

			tanim = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK70E",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"URUN",0)
			
			Doc.StatusbarMessage sira_no & ". Satir Formatlanip Yaziliyor"
			
			
			Set tbl_EBYNM222 = Doc.Select1G("d20014","STOK70T","NITELIK='XK' & EVRAKNO = '"& tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i) &"'& TARIH >= '"& Donembasi  &"' & TARIH <= '"& Donemsonu &"'","","EVRAKNO+SF_SF_UNIT",-1)
			For zz = 1 to tbl_EBYNM222.GetRecCount()
				miktar = miktar &" "& tbl_EBYNM222.SF_MIKTAR(zz) &" "& tbl_EBYNM222.SF_SF_UNIT(zz)
			Next
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4, 2, 12, 3.71, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlCenter,"",  AlanUzunluklari(sira_no, tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"SIRANO",       "CFLAG2","L"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4, 3, 12, 11.86, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"dd.mm.yyyy",      tbl_EBEYNM21.TARIH)
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4, 4, 12, 7.86, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",   AlanUzunluklari(ftserino, tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_SERINO",       "CFLAG3","L"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4, 5, 12, 10.29, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",   AlanUzunluklari(ftsirano, tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_SIRANO",        "CFLAG4","L"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4, 6, 12, 20,  "Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",   AlanUzunluklari(chkoduadi, tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_SATICI_UNVAN",  "CFLAG5","L"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4, 7, 12, 14,  "Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",   AlanUzunluklari(vergino , tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_ARACI_FRM_VDNTCNO", "CFLAG6","L"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4, 8, 12, 16.29, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",   AlanUzunluklari(tanim,  tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_MAL_V_HIZMETCINSI", "CFLAG7","L"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4, 9, 12, 11.14, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",   AlanUzunluklari(miktar , tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_MAL_V_HIZMETMIKTR", "CFLAG8","L"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4,10, 12, 15,  "Arial", 9, True, True, xlRight,"#,##0.00", AlanUzunluklari(KDVharic , tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_IVD_KH_TOPLAM",  "CFLAG9","R"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4,11, 12, 15,  "Arial", 9, True, True, xlRight,"#,##0.00", AlanUzunluklari(KDV,  tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_KDV_TUTARI",  "CFLAG10","R"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4,12, 12, 15,  "Arial", 9, True, True, xlCenter,"@",  AlanUzunluklari(gcbtarih , tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_TARIHI",   "CFLAG11","L"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4,13, 12, 13.57, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",   AlanUzunluklari(gcbno ,  tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_GCBNO",   "CFLAG12","L"))
		Next
		KDVharic_TOPLAM = KDVharic_TOPLAM + KDVharic
		KDV_TUTARI = KDV_TUTARI + KDV
		
	next
	
	tbl_EBEYNM21.SetCurrentRow 1
	
	FT_IVD_KH_TOPLAM=Doc.dRound(KDVharic_TOPLAM)
	FT_KDV_TUTARI=Doc.dRound(KDV_TUTARI)
	
	Dim Sum1
	Dim Sum2
	
	Sum1="=SUM(J5:J@)"
	Sum2="=SUM(K5:K@)"
	
'	Sum1=RePlace(Sum1,"J@","J"&i+4-1)
'	Sum2=RePlace(Sum2,"K@","K"&i+4-1)

	Sum1 = FT_IVD_KH_TOPLAM
	Sum2 = FT_KDV_TUTARI
	
	Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no+5,  9, 12, 11.14,"Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"","TOPLAM")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no+5, 10, 12, 15, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlRight,"#,##0.00",Sum1)
	Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no+5, 11, 12, 15, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlRight,"#,##0.00",Sum2)
	
End Sub


Function CreateIfNotExistDir(vDr)
	Dim fs
	Dim newfolder
	CreateIfNotExistDir=False
	Set fs=CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	if Not fs.FolderExists(vDr) then
		Set newfolder=fs.CreateFolder(vDr)
		if Not IsObject(newfolder) then
			CreateIfNotExistDir=TRUE
			Doc.d7MsgBox vDr + " Dizin Oluþturulamiyor.","Freedom",MB_OK
			Exit Function
		end if
	end if
	set fs=nothing
End Function

Sub WriteCel(ObjSheet,RowNum, ColNum, pRowHeight, pColumnWidth, FontName, FontSize, FontBold, pWrapText,pHorizontalAlignment,pNumberFormat,pAciklama)
	With ObjSheet.Cells(RowNum, ColNum)
	if Trim(pNumberFormat)<>"" then
		.NumberFormat = pNumberFormat
	end if
	'        .FormulaR1C1 = pAciklama
	.Formula = pAciklama
	'        .RowHeight = pRowHeight '76.75
	.HorizontalAlignment = pHorizontalAlignment 'xlCenter
	.VerticalAlignment = xlCenter
	.WrapText = pWrapText 'True
	.Orientation = 0
	.AddIndent = False
	.IndentLevel = 0
	.ShrinkToFit = False
	.ReadingOrder = xlContext
	.MergeCells = False
	.Font.Name = FontName '"Arial"
	.Font.Size = FontSize '9
	.Font.Bold = FontBold 'True
	.ColumnWidth = pColumnWidth '3.71
	End With
	With ObjSheet.Cells(RowNum, ColNum).Borders(xlEdgeLeft)
	.LineStyle = xlContinuous
	.Weight = xlThin
	.ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	End With
	With ObjSheet.Cells(RowNum, ColNum).Borders(xlEdgeTop)
	.LineStyle = xlContinuous
	.Weight = xlThin
	.ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	End With
	With ObjSheet.Cells(RowNum, ColNum).Borders(xlEdgeBottom)
	.LineStyle = xlContinuous
	.Weight = xlThin
	.ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	End With
	With ObjSheet.Cells(RowNum, ColNum).Borders(xlEdgeRight)
	.LineStyle = xlContinuous
	.Weight = xlThin
	.ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	End With
	
End Sub


Function AlanUzunluklari(XmlValue,tbl_T,i,tbl_L,LenFieldName,FlagFieldName,JusTify)
	Dim FieldLen
	Dim Fieldflag
	AlanUzunluklari="" & XmlValue
	AlanUzunluklari=Trim(AlanUzunluklari)
	AlanUzunluklari=RePlace(AlanUzunluklari,Space(2),Space(1))
	AlanUzunluklari=RePlace(AlanUzunluklari,Space(2),Space(1))
	
	if Len(AlanUzunluklari)<=0 then Exit Function
	
	FieldLen=tbl_L.GetFieldValue(LenFieldName,-1)
	Fieldflag=tbl_L.GetFieldValue(FlagFieldName,-1)
	
	if FieldLen=0 then Exit Function
	if Trim(Fieldflag)="1" then
		if JusTify="R" then
			AlanUzunluklari=Right(AlanUzunluklari,FieldLen)
		elseif JusTify="L" then
			AlanUzunluklari=Left(AlanUzunluklari,FieldLen)
		end if
		Exit Function
	end if
	
	if Len(AlanUzunluklari)>FieldLen then
		Doc.d7MsgBox i & ". Satir. Belirtilen Alan("+LenFieldName+") Uzunluðu=" & FieldLen & " Yazilmaya Çaliþilan Bilginin Uzunluðu=" & Len(AlanUzunluklari),"Freedom",MB_OK
		AlanUzunluklari=" "
	end if
End Function


Sub DeleteFile(FileName)
	dim fs
	Set fs=CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	if fs.FileExists(FileName) then
		fs.DeleteFile(FileName)
	end if
	set fs=nothing
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set B01E = Doc.Select1("xxxx", "BOMU01E", "", "", -1)
	KayitSayisi = B01E.GetRecCount
	Set B1T = Doc.GetTableObject("BOMU01T")
	Set B1E = Doc.GetTableObject("BOMU01E")
	For i = 1 To KayitSayisi
		EvrakNo = Trim(B01E.EVRAKNO(i))
		Doc.Load_Voucher EvrakNo
		EsasMiktar = B1E.MAMULMIKTAR
		TabloKayitSayisi = B1T.GetRecCount
		For j = 1 To TabloKayitSayisi
			If B1T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(j) = "I" Then
				B1T.BOMREC_YMAMULPS(j) = "1" 
				B1T.BOMREC_YMAMULPM(j) = EsasMiktar
			End If
		Next
		Doc.Save_Voucher
	Next
End Sub

FORM STOK26 SCROLLBARS=NO
SET CAPTION="Depodan Depoya Stok Transfer Fiþi"
SET AUTOFORMSIZE_RIGHTMARGIN=1

SET DEFAULT_FSTYLE=3

DEFINEFONT ID="default_font" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8
DEFINEFONT ID="default_text_font" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8 
DEFINEFONT ID="default_edittext_font" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8 
DEFINEFONT ID="default_button_font" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8 
DEFINEFONT ID="default_checkbox_font" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8 BOLD=Y
DEFINEFONT ID="default_groupbox_font" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8 BOLD=Y
DEFINEFONT ID="default_tabcaption_font" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8 

DEFINEFONT ID="text_font01" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8

SET DEFAULTFONT=default_font
SET DEFAULTFONT_TEXT=default_text_font
SET DEFAULTFONT_EDITTEXT=default_edittext_font
SET DEFAULTFONT_BUTTON=default_button_font
SET DEFAULTFONT_CHECKBOX=default_checkbox_font
SET DEFAULTFONT_GROUPBOX=default_groupbox_font
SET DEFAULTFONT_TABCAPTION=default_tabcaption_font

DEFINEFONT ID="FONT010" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8 BOLD=Y
DEFINEFONT ID="FONT011" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8 

DEFINEFONT ID="defaultfont_selectedtabcaption" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8 BOLD=Y
DEFINEFONT ID="defaultfont_unselectedtabcaption" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8

SET DEFAULTFONT_SELECTEDTABCAPTION=defaultfont_selectedtabcaption
SET DEFAULTFONT_UNSELECTEDTABCAPTION=defaultfont_unselectedtabcaption

 MENUITEM TEXT="Ürün Aðacý Hesaplatma (Ýlk Seviye)", ID=RECETE_HESAPLA_STD
 MENUITEM TEXT="Ürün Aðacý Hesaplatma (Tüm Seviyeler)", ID=RECETE_HESAPLA_STD_ENALT_SEVIYEYE_KADAR
 SEPARATOR
 MENUITEM TEXT="Malzemelerin Lot, Seri, F/O vs Bilgilerini Bul", ID=HESAPLANMIS_RECETENIN_LOT_SERI_BUL
 SEPARATOR
 MENUITEM TEXT="Envanter ve Ürün Aðacý Ýþlemleri", ID=USR_SENT_STOK01MULTISELECT_USE1
 SEPARATOR
 MENUITEM TEXT="Malzeme Listesinden Kopyala" ID=USR_SENT_STOK19_USE1
 MENUITEM TEXT="Paket/Koli Seçimi" ID=USR_SENT_STOK67_USE1
 SEPARATOR
 MENUITEM T="Kullanýcý Tanýmlý Alanlar" ID="MT_EDIT_UDF()"
 MENUITEM T="Mesajlar" ID="MT_EDIT_LMSG()"

ENDMENUDEF

 POPUP TEXT="&Dosya"

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DUNDAR_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : PERSONEL3
Hangi Tablo için : PERSM0
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : PERSONEL VERGÝ MATRAHI GÝRME
Kod : 
Sub IseGirisBilgileriDoldur(ByVal ExcelSheet,ByVal Satir,ByVal PersonelNo)
	Dim Tbl_PERSMBT5
	Doc.RunCommand "CMD_CALISMA_UCRET_YENI_GIRIS_BASLA"
	Set Tbl_PERSMBT2=Doc.GetTableObject("PERSM0BT5")

	Dim AlanIsmi
	Dim AlanDegeri
	Dim Sutun
	For Sutun=2 To 20
		AlanIsmi=Trim(ExcelSheet.Cells(1, Sutun))
		AlanDegeri=ExcelSheet.Cells(Satir, Sutun)
		If AlanIsmi<>"" Then
			if trim(AlanIsmi) = "TARIH1" Then
				arr = split(AlanDegeri,"/")
				gun  = arr(0)
				ay = arr(1)
				yil = arr(2)
				AlanDegeri = yil &"/"& ay  &"/"& gun
			end if
			Tbl_PERSMBT5.SetFieldValue AlanIsmi,0,AlanDegeri
		End If
	Next

	Doc.RunCommand "CMD_CALISMA_UCRET_YENI_GIRIS_OK"
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Dim myExcel
	Dim mySheet
	Set myExcel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")

	myExcel.Workbooks.Open ("f:\CALISMA2.XLS")
	Set mySheet = myExcel.Workbooks("CALISMA2.XLS").Worksheets("CALISMA")

	Dim PersonelNo

	Dim I		
	For I=2 To 170
		PersonelNo=Trim(mySheet.Cells(I, 1))
		If PersonelNo<>"" Then
			PersonelNo=Doc.LeftJustify(PersonelNo,16)
			Doc.Load_Voucher PersonelNo
			If Trim(Doc.GetLoadedVoucher())=Trim(PersonelNo) Then
				Call IseGirisBilgileriDoldur(mySheet,I,PersonelNo)
				Doc.Save_Voucher
			Else
				MsgBox(Trim(PersonelNo) & " Nolu Personel Bulunamadý")
			End If
		End If
	Next

	myExcel.Quit

	Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu

Hangi Tablo için : STOK00

Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("f:\DONUSUM.xls")
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)

	Set S00 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK00")
	Set ddd = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	For i = 2 to 200

		PersonelKodu = Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value
		if trim(PersonelKodu) = "" Then Exit For


		Doc.Load_Voucher PersonelKodu
		S00.CONVUNITS_COEF_1=Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value
		S00.CONVUNITS_1=Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value
		S00.UNITS_1=Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value
		S00.UNITS_TYPE_1_1=Sh1.Cells(i,5).Value
		S00.UNITS_TYPE_1_2=Sh1.Cells(i,6).Value
		S00.UNITS_TYPE_1_3=Sh1.Cells(i,7).Value
		

		Doc.Save_Voucher

	Next
	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
End Sub

Sub EvrakAc()
	Set KTTPERZMT = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_PERZMT")
	SatirNo=KTTPERZMT.GetCurrentRow()
	StokKodu = Trim(KTTPERZMT.ALAN1(SatirNo))
	Aciklama = Trim(KTTPERZMT.ALAN2(SatirNo))
	ModelNo/SeriNo = Trim(KTTPERZMT.ALAN3(SatirNo))
	TeslimTarihi = Trim(KTTPERZMT.ALAN4(SatirNo))
	IadeTarihi = Trim(KTTPERZMT.ALAN5(SatirNo))
Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentUI("STOK00", "")
  Doc1.Load_Voucher STOK00
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        

Ekran Formu
Uygulamalar Ýçin Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Ayarlarý

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DUNDAR_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : UDEF-9
Hangi Tablo için : UDEF00
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Work Flow Ekle
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	ProgramArray = Array("K00700", "K00701", "K00713R", "K00710", "K00711", "K00712", "REP17S", "REP17T")
	UDEFT1.SafePack
	For each Program in ProgramArray
		Set UT1 = Doc.Select2("sfdsdf", "UDEFT9", "PROGRAMKODU='"+Program+"'", "", -1)
		if UT1.GetRowCount = 0 Then
			UDEFT1.AddRow()
			satirNo = UDEFT1.GetRowCount()
			UDEFT1.PROGRAMKODU(satirNo) = Program
			UDEFT1.RIGHT_X(satirNo) = "1"
			UDEFT1.RIGHT_R(satirNo) = "1"
			UDEFT1.RIGHT_W(satirNo) = "1"
			UDEFT1.RIGHT_U(satirNo) = "1"
			UDEFT1.RIGHT_D(satirNo) = "1"
		End if
	Next
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DUNDAR_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : SIPAL2
Hangi Tablo için : OTOSP1
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : EXCELDEN ÖNGÖRÜ SÝPARÝÞÝNE AKTARMA
Kod : 
Public Dosya, TabloS, Tablo, OTOSE, OTOSL

Sub Makro1()
	Set Tablo = Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	Tablo.AddField2 "DOSYA","CHAR",40,0,"L","F2=DO_SYSBRW_FILENAME()",""
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
	Dosya = trim(Tablo.DOSYA)
End Sub

Sub SiparisKaydet()
	Dim vTable, TempTable

	Set OTOSE = Doc.GetTableObject("OTOSP1E")
	Set OTOSL = Doc.GetTableObject("OTOSP1L")
	Set TabloS = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")	

	Dosya = TabloS.DOSYA
	Cari = "320 01 01 003"

	if trim(Dosya)="" then Exit Sub

	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb = Excel.Workbooks.Open(Trim(Dosya))
	Set Sh1 = Wb.Sheets(1)

	' Yeni Sipariþi Aç
	Nret = Doc.New_Voucher()
	OTOSL.Empty()
	OTOSE.TARIH = Doc.Bugun()
	OTOSE.CH_KODU = Trim(Cari)

	' ----------- Excel Tablodan Trh ve Miktarlarý oku ----------

	For i=6 To 65000
		If trim(Sh1.Cells(i, 2).value)="" Then Exit For
		StokKodu = Trim(Sh1.Cells(i, 2).value)
			For j=8 To 250 	
				If trim(Sh1.Cells(4, j).value)="Toplam Ýhtiyaç" Then Exit For
				xTeslimTarihi = Trim(Mid(Cstr(Sh1.Cells(4, j).value),3))
				TTarihGun = Left(xTeslimTarihi,2)
				TTarihAy  = Mid(xTeslimTarihi,4,2)
				TTarihYil1 = Right(xTeslimTarihi,2)
				TTarihYil = "20"+TTarihYil1
'				d7Tarih = Day(TTarihGun) &"/"& Month(TTarihAy) &"/"& Year(TTarihYil)
				TTTarih = Trim(TTarihGun) &"/"& Trim(TTarihAy) &"/"& Trim(TTarihYil)
				SipMiktari = Sh1.Cells(i, j).value
				SiparisMiktari = Doc.Round(SipMiktari,0)
				If SiparisMiktari>0 Then
					OTOSL.AddRow()
					SatirNo = OTOSL.GetRecCount()
					OTOSL.SetCurrentRow(SatirNo)
					OTOSL.KOD(satirNo) = Trim(StokKodu)
					OTOSL.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = Doc.Cdbl(SiparisMiktari)
					OTOSL.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = Trim(TTTarih)
					OTOSL.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo)=Trim(TTTarih)
				End If
		Next
	Next

	' Sipariþi Kaydet 
	nret = Doc.Save_Voucher()

	' Ekraný boþalt
	' nret = Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher()

	Excel.Quit
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
	MsgBox("Ýþlem Tamam")
End Sub

Makro Kodu : FREEMAIL
Hangi Tablo için : 
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : EMAÝL GÖNDERMELERDE KULLANILACAK MAKRO
Kod : 
Sub SendMail(strTo,strHeader,strMsg,EmailGonderildimi)

	'On Error Resume Next
	cdoSendUsingPickup = 1 
	cdoSendUsingPort = 2 
	cdoAnonymous = 0
	cdoBasic = 1
	cdoNTLM = 2 'NTLM

	mailserver = "89.19.10.108"
	mailUser = "bilgiislem@dundarlarmakina.com"
	mailPass = "dm2675bi"
	mailFrom = mailUser
	
	Set objMessage = CreateObject("CDO.Message")
	objMessage.From = trim(mailFrom)
	objMessage.To = strTo
	objMessage.Subject = strHeader

	If InStr(1, strMsg, "<html") > 0 and InStr(1, strMsg, "</html>") > 0 Then
		objMessage.HTMLBody = strMsg
	Else
		objMessage.TextBody = strMsg
	End if
	
	if trim(DosyaAdi) <> "" Then
		objMessage.AddAttachment("file://" & DosyaAdi)
	End if
	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/sendusing") = cdoSendUsingPort 
	
	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpserver") = trim(mailserver)
	
	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpauthenticate") = cdoBasic
	
	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/sendusername") = trim(mailUser)
	
	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/sendpassword") = trim(mailPass)
	
	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpserverport") = 587
	
	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpusessl") = False
	
	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpconnectiontimeout") = 60
	
	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Update
	objMessage.Send
	
	EmailGonderildimi = ""
	Hata = Err.Number
	if Hata > 0 Then
		Doc.d7msgBox "Mail Gönderme Hatasý : " & Hata & vbcrlf & Err.Description, "Mail Gönderilemedi", MB_OK
		SendMail = Hata
	Else
		Msgbox "Talep Durum e-maili baþarýyla gönderildi."
		EmailGonderildimi = "E"
	End if
End Sub

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DUNDAR_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : F2-S46-1-1
Hangi Tablo için : 
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Satýþ Sipariþi- TAB = Satýþ Sipariþi - <KOD> ALANI ÝÇÝN F2
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD1","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD2","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD3","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD4","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TUMRESIMNODOLU", "CHAR", 1, 0, "L", "", ""
	MTable.Addfield2 "RESIMNOAYNI", "CHAR", 1, 0, "L", "", ""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SATINALINAN", "CHAR", 1, 0, "L", "", ""
	MTable.Addfield2 "RESIMNO", "CHAR", 24, 0, "L", "", ""
	
	Dim MTable2
	Set MTable2=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLE2=STACK","IO")
	MTable2.Addfield2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_1","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_2","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_3","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_4","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_5","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_6","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_7","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_8","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_9","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_10","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "RESIMNO","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""

	MTable.SATINALINAN = "H"
	MTable.TUMRESIMNODOLU = "H"
	MTable.RESIMNOAYNI = "H"

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU2",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Suz()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE2")
	MTable2.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTable2.Empty
	
	If MTable.SATINALINAN = "H" AND MTable.TUMRESIMNODOLU = "H" AND MTable.RESIMNOAYNI = "H" AND Trim(MTable.KOD) = "" AND Trim(MTable.AD1) = "" AND Trim(MTable.AD2) = "" AND Trim(MTable.RESIMNO) = "" Then
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
	If MTable.SATINALINAN = "E" Then 
		Kriter1 = "KART_TIPI = 'S'"
	Else
		Kriter1 = "KART_TIPI <> ' '"
	End If
	
	If MTable.TUMRESIMNODOLU = "E" Then 
		Kriter2 = "SUPPLIERCODE <> ' '"
	Else
		Kriter2 = "SUPPLIERCODE <> '56564664546GTGDFGG4T45THRH'"
	End If

	If MTable.RESIMNOAYNI = "E" Then 
		MTable.TUMRESIMNODOLU = "H"
		MTable.SATINALINAN = "H"
		MTable.KOD = ""
		MTable.AD1 = ""
		MTable.AD2 = ""
		MTable.AD3 = ""
		MTable.AD4 = ""
		MTable.RESIMNO = ""
		
			QStr = "SELECT *"  & _
					" FROM STOK00"  & _
					" WHERE SUPPLIERCODE  <> ' ' AND " & "SUPPLIERCODE IN (SELECT SUPPLIERCODE FROM STOK00 GROUP BY SUPPLIERCODE HAVING COUNT(*) > 1) ORDER BY SUPPLIERCODE DESC"
	Else
		MTable.RESIMNOAYNI = ""
		QStr = "SELECT *"  & _
					" FROM STOK00"  & _
					" WHERE 1=1 AND " & Kriter1 & "AND " & Kriter2
					
		If Trim(MTable.KOD) <> "" Then
			QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.KOD) LIKE '%" & UCase(Trim(MTable.KOD)) & "%'"
		End If
		
		If Trim(MTable.AD1) <> "" Then
			QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(Trim(MTable.AD1)) & "%'"
		End If

		If Trim(MTable.AD2) <> "" Then
			QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(Trim(MTable.AD2)) & "%'"
		End If

		If Trim(MTable.AD3) <> "" Then
			QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(Trim(MTable.AD3)) & "%'"
		End If
		
		If Trim(MTable.AD4) <> "" Then
			QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(Trim(MTable.AD4)) & "%'"
		End If
		
		If Trim(MTable.RESIMNO) <> "" Then
			QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.SUPPLIERCODE) LIKE '%" & UCase(Trim(MTable.RESIMNO)) & "%'"
			QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY SUPPLIERCODE DESC"
		End If
	End If
 
	Doc.RunSQLQuery "XXXX_1", QStr
	Set S00 = Doc.GetTableobject("XXXX_1")
	For i = 1 to S00.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Listeye Ekleniyor : " & i & " / " & S00.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTable2.AddRow()
		MTable2.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTable2.KOD = S00.KOD(i)
		MTable2.AD = S00.AD(i)
		MTable2.GK_1 = S00.GK_1(i)
		MTable2.GK_2 = S00.GK_2(i)
		MTable2.GK_3 = S00.GK_3(i)
		MTable2.GK_4 = S00.GK_4(i)
		MTable2.GK_5 = S00.GK_5(i)
		MTable2.GK_6 = S00.GK_6(i)
		MTable2.GK_7 = S00.GK_7(i)
		MTable2.GK_8 = S00.GK_8(i)
		MTable2.GK_9 = S00.GK_9(i)
		MTable2.GK_10 = S00.GK_10(i)
		MTable2.RESIMNO= S00.SUPPLIERCODE(i)
	Next
	MTable2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	MTable2.SetCurrentRow(1)
End Sub
'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Sec()
	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE2")
	'Satir = Mtable2.GetCurrentRow
	'StokKodu = Trim(MTable2.KOD(Satir))
	StokKodu = MTable2.KOD
	If StokKodu = "" Then Exit Sub
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
	Set T1 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK46T")
	T1.KOD = StokKodu
End Sub

Sub Sifirla
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE2")
	MTable.Empty
	MTable2.Empty
	MTable.SATINALINAN = "H"
	MTable.TUMRESIMNODOLU = "H"
	MTable.RESIMNOAYNI = "H"
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("f:\CALISMA2.xls")
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)

	Set S00 = Doc.GetTableObject("PERSM0BT5")
	Set ddd = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	For i = 2 to 180
		PersonelKodu = Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value
		if trim(PersonelKodu) = "" Then Exit For


		Doc.Load_Voucher PersonelKodu
		S00.YIL=Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value
		S00.AY=Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value
		S00.GVMATRAHI=Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value

		

		Doc.Save_Voucher

	Next
	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set ListeTablosu = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK46T_LISTESI2")
	For i = 1 to ListeTablosu.GetRowCount()
		Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK46", "")
		Set S46E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK46E0")
		Set S46T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK46T")
		Evrakno = Trim(ListeTablosu.EVRAKNO(i))
		ArtikelNumarasi = ListeTablosu.ARTNO(i)
		Doc1.Load_Voucher Evrakno
		For j = 1 To S46T.GetRecCount
			If S46T.ARTNO(j) = ArtikelNumarasi Then S46T.ACIKKAPALI(j) = "K"			
		Next
		Doc1.Save_Voucher
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Next
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DUNDAR_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : SIPAKT
Hangi Tablo için : OTOSP1
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : (OT)Öngörü sipariþinin belirli tarih aralýðýný satýþ sipariþine aktarma-(Önceki sipariþleri kapatýr)
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	if trim(OTOSP1E.KSN_TARIH1) = "" or trim(OTOSP1E.KSN_TARIH2) = "" Then
		k = Doc.d7MsgBox("Kesin Sipariþ tarih aralýðý girmelisiniz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK)
		Exit Sub
	End if
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK40", "")
	Set S40E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set S40T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	
	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S40E.MUSTERIKODU = OTOSP1E.CH_KODU
	For i = 1 to OTOSP1L.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Sipariþe aktarýlýyor : (%" & Doc.Round(i /  OTOSP1L.GetRowCount() * 100,0) & ")"
		if OTOSP1L.REV_RTESTARIH(i) >= OTOSP1E.KSN_TARIH1 and OTOSP1L.REV_RTESTARIH(i) <= OTOSP1E.KSN_TARIH2 Then
			S40T.AddRow()
			satirNo = S40T.GetRowCount()
			S40T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			S40T.KOD(satirNo) = OTOSP1L.KOD(i)
			S40T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = OTOSP1L.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			S40T.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = OTOSP1L.REV_RTESTARIH(i)
			S40T.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = OTOSP1L.REV_RTESTARIH(i)
			OTOSP1L.ACIKKAPALI2(i) = "K"
		End if
	Next
	if Doc1.Save_Voucher = 0 Then Call OncekiSiparisleriKapat(S40E.EVRAKNO)
	Doc.Save_Voucher
	k = Doc.d7MsgBox(trim(S40E.EVRAKNO) & " No.lu sipariþ yaratýldý", "Ýþlem tamamlandý", MB_OK)
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
End Sub
Sub OncekiSiparisleriKapat(SiparisNo)
	Doc.StatusBarMessage "Önceki sipariþler kapatýlýyor..."
	Set S40T = Doc.Select1("w2www", "STOK40T", "ACIKKAPALI=' '&MUSTERIKODU='"+OTOSP1E.CH_KODU+"'&EVRAKNO<>'" & SiparisNo & "'", "", -1)
	if S40T.GetRowCount = 0 Then Exit Sub
	S40T.GroupBy("EVRAKNO")
	'KodArray = GetKodList()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK40", "")
	Set ST40E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set ST40T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	Bulunan = 0
	For i = 1 to S40T.GetRowCount()
		if Bulunan > 0 Then Doc1.Save_Voucher
		Bulunan = 0
		Doc1.Load_Voucher S40T.EVRAKNO(i)
		For j = 1 to ST40T.GetRowCount()
			Doc.StatusBarMessage "Önceki sipariþler kapatýlýyor : " & S40T.EVRAKNO(i) & " - " & j & " / " & ST40T.GetRowCount()
			'sa = Filter(KodArray, trim(ST40T.KOD(j)), True)
			'if uBound(sa) <> -1 Then
			if true then
				Bulunan = Bulunan + 1
				if ST40T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK(j) = 0 Then
					ST40T.KOD(j) = ""
					ST40T.SF_SF_UNIT(j) = ""
					ST40T.RTESTARIH(j) = ""
				Else
					ST40T.ACIKKAPALI(j) = "K"
				End if
			End if
		Next
	Next
	if Bulunan > 0 Then Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	'doc.DeleteDocumentNoUI "STOK40", ""
	'Doc.DeleteTableObject "w2www"
End Sub

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DUNDAR_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : SIPAL
Hangi Tablo için : OTOSP1
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : (OT) Excelden alma ve öngöüye kadetme
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()

	Set May_container = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLE2=STACK","IO")

	May_container.Addfield2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	May_container.Addfield2 "TARIH","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	May_container.Addfield2 "DURUM","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	May_container.Addfield2 "MITAR","DOUBLE",22,2,"R","",""

	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")

	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open(trim(OTOSP1E.FULLFILENAME1), False, True)

	Dim k,j
	for i = 1 To Wb1.Worksheets.Count

		k = 0
		j = 31

	s_kodu_al = trim(StokKodunuAl(Wb1.Sheets(i).Name))

	if s_kodu_al <> "" then
	
		Do While k <= 1

			satirNo = May_container.AddRow()
			May_container.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			d_tarih =  trim(Wb1.Sheets(i).Cells(j,2).value)
			
			May_container.KOD = s_kodu_al
			May_container.TARIH = TarihFormat(d_tarih)
			May_container.DURUM = Wb1.Sheets(i).Cells(j,5).value
			May_container.MIKTAR = Wb1.Sheets(i).Cells(j,6).value

			j = j + 1
			if trim(Wb1.Sheets(i).Cells(j,2).value	) = "" then k = 2

			Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'den Aktarýlýyor : " & j
		Loop
	end if

	next

	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
	
	for  t = 1 To May_container.GetRecCount()

		if  trim(May_container.KOD(t)) <> "" and  May_container.MIKTAR(t) > 0then
			
			satirNo = OTOSP1L.AddRow
			OTOSP1L.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			OTOSP1L.Kod = May_container.KOD(t)
			OTOSP1L.SF_MIKTAR = May_container.MIKTAR(t)
			OTOSP1L.RTESTARIH = May_container.TARIH(t)
			OTOSP1L.DOCK_CODE = May_container.DURUM(t)

			if UCase(Trim(May_container.DURUM(t))) = "P" then
				OTOSP1L.ATTRIB = "H"
			elseif UCase(Trim(May_container.DURUM(t))) = "F" then
				OTOSP1L.ATTRIB = "G" 	
			end if 


		end if
			Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & t
	next

	if Doc.Save_Voucher  <> 0 and 5 = 3 then
		k = OTOSP1L.GetRecCount()
		Do While k >= 1 
			OTOSP1L.DeleteRow_NOUI(k)
			k = k - 1
		Loop
	end if

end sub

function TarihFormat(byref v_tarih)

	TarihFormat = ""
	if Len(trim(v_tarih)) > 0 then

		tar_ih = split(v_tarih, "/")
		if Len(trim(tar_ih(0))) = 1 then tar_ih(0) = "0" & tar_ih(0)
		if Len(trim(tar_ih(1))) = 1 then tar_ih(1) = "0" & tar_ih(1)
		TarihFormat = 	tar_ih(1) &"/"& tar_ih(0) &"/"& tar_ih(2)	

	end if	

end function

function StokKodunuAl(v_m_kodu)

	StokKodunuAl = ""

	Set A_STOK0GT = Doc.Select1("dsfasd4r3", "STOK0GT", "KOD = '" & OTOSP1E.CH_KODU & "'", "", -1)

	if A_STOK0GT.GetRecCount() < 0 then Exit function
	
	for i = 1 To A_STOK0GT.GetRecCount()

		if trim(A_STOK0GT.MSTOKCODE(i)) = trim(v_m_kodu) then
			StokKodunuAl = A_STOK0GT.STOKCODE(i)
		end if

	next 
end function

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DUNDAR_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : SIPAL-2
Hangi Tablo için : OTOSP1
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : (OT) Eski Öngörüleri Kapatýp Yeni Sipariþleri Açmak
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK40","")
	Set ST40E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set ST40T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40T")

	Flag = False
	for i = 1 To OTOSP1L.GetRecCount()	
		if OTOSP1L.ATTRIB(i) <> "H"  then
			OTOSP1L.ACIKKAPALI2(i) = "K"
			Flag = True
		end if 
	next

	
	if Flag		then 

		Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK40","")
		Set ST40E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
		Set ST40T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40T")

		nret = Doc1.New_Voucher()
		ST40T.Empty

		ST40E.MUSTERIKODU = OTOSP1E.CH_KODU
		ST40E.GK_1="08"
		for i = 1 To OTOSP1L.GetRecCount()	
			if OTOSP1L.ATTRIB(i) <> "H"  then
				satirNo = ST40T.AddRow
				ST40T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				ST40T.KOD(satirNo) = OTOSP1L.KOD(i)
				ST40T.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = OTOSP1L.RTESTARIH(i)
				ST40T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = OTOSP1L.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				ST40T.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo)= 	OTOSP1L.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
				OTOSP1L.ACIKKAPALI2(i) = "K"
			end if 
		next

		nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	end if


	Call EskiMusteriOngoruleriniKapat()

End Sub


Sub EskiMusteriOngoruleriniKapat()

	Set A_OTOSP1E = Doc.Select1("TU5HF", "OTOSP1E", "EVRAKNO<>'" & OTOSP1E.EVRAKNO & "'&CH_KODU = '"& OTOSP1E.CH_KODU &"'& ACIKKAPALI <> 'K' ", "", -1)

	if A_OTOSP1E.GetRecCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
		
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("OTOSP1","")
	Set OTOE = Doc1.GetTableObject("OTOSP1E")
	Set OTOL = Doc1.GetTableObject("OTOSP1L")
		
	for t = 1 To A_OTOSP1E.GetRecCount()		

		nret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(A_OTOSP1E.EVRAKNO(t))

		OTOE.ACIKKAPALI = "K"


		nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
		nret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	Next
End Sub

Sub FRM_STOK40T_KRITERLER

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub SiparisleriGetir2
	Set KRITERS = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40T_KRITERLER")
	MPSNO_B_DEGERI = Trim(KRITERS.MPSNO_B)
	MPSNO_E_DEGERI = Trim(KRITERS.MPSNO_E)

	STANO_B_DEGERI = Trim(KRITERS.STANO_B)
	STANO_E_DEGERI = Trim(KRITERS.STANO_E)	
		
	Qstr = "SELECT * FROM "
	Qstr = Qstr & " ( "
	Qstr = Qstr & " (SELECT EVRAKNO, MUSTERIKODU, KOD, SF_MIKTAR, ARTNO FROM STOK40T) S40T "
	Qstr = Qstr & " LEFT JOIN "
	Qstr = Qstr & " (SELECT MPSNO, STANO, KTA_KEY FROM KTA_STOK40T) KTAS40T "
	Qstr = Qstr & " ON S40T.ARTNO=KTAS40T.KTA_KEY "
	Qstr = Qstr & " ) "
	Qstr = Qstr & " WHERE 1=1 "

	If Trim(MPSNO_B_DEGERI) <> "" AND Trim(MPSNO_E_DEGERI) <> "" Then
		Qstr = Qstr & " AND (Trim(MPSNO) >= '"&MPSNO_B_DEGERI&"' AND Trim(MPSNO) <= '"&MPSNO_E_DEGERI&"') "
	ElseIf Trim(MPSNO_B_DEGERI) <> "" Then
		Qstr = Qstr & " AND Trim(MPSNO) >= '"&MPSNO_B_DEGERI&"' "
	ElseIf Trim(MPSNO_E_DEGERI) <> "" Then 
		Qstr = Qstr & " AND Trim(MPSNO) <= '"&MPSNO_E_DEGERI&"' "
	End If

	If Trim(STANO_B_DEGERI) <> "" AND Trim(STANO_E_DEGERI) <> "" Then
		Qstr = Qstr & " AND (Trim(STANO) >= '"&STANO_B_DEGERI&"' AND Trim(STANO) <= '"&STANO_E_DEGERI&"') "
	ElseIf Trim(STANO_B_DEGERI) <> "" Then
		Qstr = Qstr & " AND Trim(STANO) >= '"&STANO_B_DEGERI&"' "
	ElseIf Trim(STANO_E_DEGERI) <> "" Then 
		Qstr = Qstr & " AND Trim(STANO) <= '"&STANO_E_DEGERI&"' "
	End If

	Qstr = Qstr & " ORDER BY EVRAKNO"

	Set SQLTbl = Doc.RunSQLQuery("SQLTbl", Qstr)
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLES")

	MTable.Empty
	MTable.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTable.AppendTable(SQLTbl)
	MTable.Refresh
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
End Sub
'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Public MTableK
Sub TabloAlanYukle
	Qstr = "SELECT * FROM "
	Qstr = Qstr & " ( "
	Qstr = Qstr & " (SELECT EVRAKNO, MUSTERIKODU, KOD, SF_MIKTAR, ARTNO FROM STOK40T) S40T "
	Qstr = Qstr & " LEFT JOIN "
	Qstr = Qstr & " (SELECT MPSNO, STANO, KTA_KEY FROM KTA_STOK40T) KTAS40T "
	Qstr = Qstr & " ON S40T.ARTNO=KTAS40T.KTA_KEY "
	Qstr = Qstr & " ) "
	Qstr = Qstr & " WHERE 1=1 "
		
	Set MTableS = Doc.RunSQLQuery("MAKROTABLES", Qstr & " AND 1=0 ")	

	Set MTableK=Doc.CreateTableObject("STOK40T_KRITERLER=STACK","IO")
	MTableK.Addfield2 "MPSNO_B", "CHAR", 10, 0, "L", "", ""
	MTableK.Addfield2 "MPSNO_E", "CHAR", 10, 0, "L", "", ""
	MTableK.Addfield2 "STANO_B", "CHAR", 10, 0, "L", "", ""
	MTableK.Addfield2 "STANO_E", "CHAR", 10, 0, "L", "", ""

End Sub
'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Makro1()

	For i = 1 to STOK40T.GetRecCount()
		AmbalajMiktari = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_1",STOK40T.KOD(i),"")
		PaketMiktari = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(STOK40T.SF_MIKTAR(i),AmbalajMiktari))
		ToplamMiktar = PaketMiktari * AmbalajMiktari
		GK2=Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","GK_2",STOK40T.KOD(i),"")
		GK2_adi=Doc.GetGecoValue("STKGK2","AD",GK2,-1)
		STOK40T.NOTES(i) = Doc.NumFormat(PaketMiktari,20,0)& "x" & Doc.NumFormat(AmbalajMiktari,20,0) & "=" & Doc.NumFormat(ToplamMiktar,20,0)&" ("&"Grup2 :"&trim(gk2_adi)&" )"
	Next
End Sub
'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Nitelik_Z2_icin_uretim_fisi_olustur
	Set S40E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set S40T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	
	Nitelik = Trim(S40E.MUHT_FATURA_NITELIK)

	If Left(Trim(S40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4), 6) = "Üretim" Then
		OncedenAcilmisUretimFisNo = Trim(Mid(Trim(S40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4), 13, 999))
		Msgbox "Daha Önce [ " & OncedenAcilmisUretimFisNo & " ] No' lu Üretim Fiþi Oluþturulmuþ."
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
	If Nitelik <> "Z2" Then
		Msgbox "Sadece Nitelik (Z2) ise Çalýþýr."
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
	S40T.SafePack
	KayitSayisi = S40T.GetRecCount
	
	If KayitSayisi = 0 Then
		Msgbox "Kayýt Bulunamadý."
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
	MusteriKodu = Trim(S40E.MUSTERIKODU)
	SatisSiparisNo = Trim(S40E.EVRAKNO)
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK20", "")
	Set S20E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Set S20T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
	
	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S20E.AMBCODE = "MMD01"
	S20E.IMALATAMBCODE = "HMD01"
	S20E.YANMAMULAMBCODE = "YY"
		
	For i = 1 To KayitSayisi
		StokKodu = Trim(S40T.KOD(i))
		Miktar = S40T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		Varyant1 = Trim(S40T.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i))
		Varyant2 = Trim(S40T.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i))
		Varyant3 = Trim(S40T.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i))
		
		satirNo = S20T.AddRow()
		S20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		
		S20T.KOD = StokKodu
		S20T.SF_MIKTAR = Miktar
		S20T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = Varyant1
		S20T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = Varyant2
		S20T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = Varyant3
		S20T.REF_TEXT_1 = "[ " & SatisSiparisNo & " ] No' lu Satýþ Sipariþinden Oluþturuldu."
	Next
	
	'Doc1.RunCommand "CMD_SIRALIK_ISLEM_003"
	'Doc1.RunCommand "CMD_STOK01DEBULUNDUMU_BILGISATIRIE"
	k=Doc1.Save_Voucher

	If k <> 0 then
		y = Doc.d7MsgBox("Üretim Fiþi Kaydedilemedi." & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem Kesilmiþtir", MB_OK)
		Exit Sub
	End if
	
	UretimFisNo = Trim(S20E.EVRAKNO)
	S40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4	= "Üretim Fiþ No : " & UretimFisNo
	Doc.Save_Voucher
	
	Msgbox UretimFisNo & " No' lu Üretim Fiþi Oluþturuldu."
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Set Doc1 = Nothing			
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DUNDAR_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : STOK46FYT
Hangi Tablo için : STOK46
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : FÝYAT LÝSTESÝNDEKÝ KODLARI SATINALMA SÝPARÝÞÝNE AKTARMA
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	
	ChKod = trim(STOK46E.CH_KODU)

	Dim Kriter
	Kriter = ""
	Kriter = Kriter&"REF_CHKODU="&ChKod

	Dim ttt
	Set ttt = Doc.Select1("ABC","STOK48T",Kriter,"",-1)

	If ttt.GetRowCount > 0 Then
	
	Dim i
		for i = 1 to ttt.GetrecCount
			Kod = ttt.KOD(i)
			StokAdi = doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK00","AD",Kod,"")
			
			STOK46T.KOD(i) = Kod
			STOK46T.KOD_STOK00_AD(i) = StokAdi
			STOK46T.OR_FIYAT(i) = ttt.PRICE(i)
			STOK46T.PRICEUNIT(i) = ttt.PRICEUNIT(i)
			STOK46T.SF_SF_UNIT(i) = ttt.SF_UNIT(i) 	
			STOK46T.AddRow()
		Next
	End if
	


End Sub

Sub OnayliTeklifleriOzetle
	Sayac = 0
	Set S47T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK47T")
	Set ST47T2 = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_S47T2")
	Set S47E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK47E")	
	Set KS47T = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_S47T")
	EvrakNo = Trim(S47E.EVRAKNO)

	Msgbox "Aktarmadan Önce Kayýt Ýþlemi Gerçekleþecektir."
	nret = Doc.Save_Voucher
	
	'Teklifler tablosunda onaylýlarý alýr
	Set KS47T = Doc.Select1("ooppoo", "KTT_S47T", "EVRAKNO='"&EvrakNo&"'&ALAN9='E'", "", -1)
	KayitSayisi = KS47T.GetRecCount

	If KayitSayisi = 0 Then 
		Msgbox "Aktarýlacak Onay Verilmiþ Satýr Bulunamadý."
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
	'Teklifler tablosunda birden fazla onay varmý kontrolü
	If S47T.GetRecCount > 0 Then
		For u = 1 To S47T.GetRecCount
			SRNUM_DEGERI = Trim(S47T.SRNUM(u))
			TRNUM_DEGERI = Trim(S47T.TRNUM(u))
			'bir satýra ait birden fazla onaylý varmý kontrolü

			Set SS47TT = Doc.Select1("EEEEDD", "KTT_S47T", "EVRAKNO='"&EvrakNo&"'&ALAN9='E'&OR_TRNUM='"&TRNUM_DEGERI&"'", "", -1)

			If SS47TT.GetRecCount > 1 Then
				Msgbox "Tedarikçinin Onaylý Tek Teklifi Olmalýdýr." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf &_
				"Aþaðýdaki Malzemeye Ait Birden Fazla Onay Verilmiþ Satir Var. " & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & Trim(S47T.KOD(u))
				Exit For
				Exit Sub
			ElseIf SS47TT.GetRecCount = 1 Then
				ORTRNUM_DEGERI = Trim(SS47TT.OR_TRNUM(1))
				'Onaylý olan teklifler daha önce sipariþe dönüþecekler tablosunda varmý kontrolü
				Set KS47T2 = Doc.Select1("ttyyrrr", "KTT_S47T2", "EVRAKNO='"&EvrakNo&"'&ALAN13='"&ORTRNUM_DEGERI&"'", "", -1)
		
				If KS47T2.GetRecCount = 0 Then
					Sayac = Sayac + 1
					ST47T2.SetRowAID 1,1,1		
					
					Set S47T_X = Doc.Select1("nnyyuu", "STOK47T", "EVRAKNO='"&EvrakNo&"'&TRNUM='"&ORTRNUM_DEGERI&"'", "", -1)
					Kod = Trim(S47T_X.KOD(1))
					TALEPARTNO = Trim(S47T_X.ARTNO(1))
					Deger_STOKNONSTOK = Trim(S47T_X.STOK_NONSTOK(1))
					Deger_STOKNONSTOKADI = Trim(S47T_X.NONSTOK_AD(1))
					StokBirimi = Trim(S47T_X.SF_SF_UNIT(1))
					
					OnaylananTedarikciKodu = Trim(SS47TT.ALAN1(1))
					OnaylananTedarikciAdi = Trim(SS47TT.ALAN2(1))
					TeklifeBazMiktar = SS47TT.ALAN3(1)
					TeslimTarihi = SS47TT.ALAN4(1)
					OdemeSekli = SS47TT.ALAN5(1)
					Gun = SS47TT.ALAN6(1)
					OnaylananFiyat = SS47TT.ALAN7(1)
					Birimi = SS47TT.ALAN8(1)
					TalepMiktari = SS47TT.ALAN31(1)
					SiparisMiktari = SS47TT.ALAN32(1)
					
					Set ST47T2 = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_S47T2")
					
					If ST47T2.GetRowCount = 0 Then
						satirNo = ST47T2.AddRow()
					Else
						ST47T2.InsertRow(1)
						satirNo = 1
					End if
	
					'SatirNo = ST47T2.AddRow			
					ST47T2.SetCurrentRow(SatirNo)
					ST47T2.ALAN1 = Kod
					StokAdi = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",Kod,""))
					ST47T2.ALAN2 = StokAdi
					ST47T2.ALAN3 = OnaylananTedarikciKodu
					ST47T2.ALAN4 = OnaylananTedarikciAdi
					ST47T2.ALAN5 = TeklifeBazMiktar
					ST47T2.ALAN50 = TalepMiktari
					ST47T2.ALAN52 = SiparisMiktari
					ST47T2.ALAN51 = StokBirimi
					ST47T2.ALAN6 = TeslimTarihi
					ST47T2.ALAN7 = OdemeSekli
					ST47T2.ALAN8 = Gun
					ST47T2.ALAN9 = OnaylananFiyat
					ST47T2.ALAN10 = Birimi
					ST47T2.ALAN13 = TRNUM_DEGERI
					ST47T2.ALAN14 = SRNUM_DEGERI
					ST47T2.ALAN15 = Deger_STOKNONSTOK
					ST47T2.ALAN16 = Deger_STOKNONSTOKADI
					ST47T2.ALAN17 = "H"
					ST47T2.ALAN18 = TALEPARTNO
				End If
			End If
		Next
		nret = Doc.Save_Voucher
		ST47T2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	End If
	
	If Sayac = 0 Then 
		Msgbox "Aktarýlacak Bir Onay Verilmiþ Satýr Bulunamadý."
		ST47T2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
End Sub

'------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub SipariseDonustur

	Set ST47E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK47E")
	EvrakNo = Trim(ST47E.EVRAKNO)

	Onay1 = Trim(ST47E.ONAY_1KULLANICI)
	If Onay1 = "" Then
		Msgbox "Onay Verilmemiþ Talep Sipariþe Aktarýlamaz." 
		Exit Sub
	End If 

	Set ST47T2_1 = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_S47T2")
	KayitSayisi = ST47T2_1.GetRecCount

	Set KTTS47T2_3 = Doc.Select1("rereref", "KTT_S47T2", "EVRAKNO='"&EvrakNo&"'&ALAN17='H'", "", -1)
	If KTTS47T2_3.GetRecCount = 0 Then 
		Msgbox "Sipariþe Dönüþecek Kayýt Bulunamadý."
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
	Set S47E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK47E")	
	EvrakNo = Trim(S47E.EVRAKNO)

	Set KTTS47T2_2 = Doc.Select1("yu23488yu", "KTT_S47T2", "EVRAKNO='"&EvrakNo&"'&ALAN11=' '", "", -1)

	KTTS47T2_2.GroupBy("ALAN3")
		
	AcilacakSiparisSayisi = KTTS47T2_2.GetRecCount
	If AcilacakSiparisSayisi = 0 Then
		Msgbox "Bu Talebin Sipariþleri Daha Önce Açýlmýþ."
		Exit Sub
	End If
		
	For i = 1 To AcilacakSiparisSayisi
		Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK46", "")
		Set S46E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK46E")	
		Set S46T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK46T")
		Set S46T1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK46T1")
		Doc1.New_Voucher()
		S46T.Empty
		S46T1.Empty
				
		CariKodu = Trim(KTTS47T2_2.ALAN3(i))
		S46E.CH_KODU = CariKodu
		
		Set KTTS47T2 = Doc.Select1("yu234yu", "KTT_S47T2", "EVRAKNO='"&EvrakNo&"'&ALAN3='"&CariKodu&"'", "", -1)
		
		For j = 1 To KTTS47T2.GetRecCount
			
			StokKodu = Trim(KTTS47T2.ALAN1(j))
			Deger_STOKNONSTOK = Trim(KTTS47T2.ALAN15(j))
			Deger_STOKNONSTOKADI = Trim(KTTS47T2.ALAN16(j))
			TalepMiktari = KTTS47T2.ALAN50(j)
			SiparisMiktari =	 KTTS47T2.ALAN52(j)	
			StokBirimi = Trim(KTTS47T2.ALAN51(j))
			TeslimTarihi = KTTS47T2.ALAN6(j)
			OdemeSekli = Trim(KTTS47T2.ALAN7(j))
			Gun = KTTS47T2.ALAN8(j)
			TeklifFiyati = KTTS47T2.ALAN9(j)
			Birimi = KTTS47T2.ALAN10(j)
				
			SatirNo = S46T.AddRow
			S46T.SetCurrentRow(SatirNo)
			S46T.KOD = StokKodu
			S46T.SF_MIKTAR = SiparisMiktari
			S46T.SF_SF_UNIT = StokBirimi
			S46T.RTESTARIH = TeslimTarihi
			S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_1 = OdemeSekli
			S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_NUM_1 = Gun
			S46T.OR_FIYAT = TeklifFiyati
			S46T.PRICEUNIT = Birimi
			S46T.STOK_NONSTOK = Deger_STOKNONSTOK
			S46T.NONSTOK_AD = Deger_STOKNONSTOKADI

			SatirNo = S46T1.AddRow
			If S46T1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
				satirNo = S46T1.AddRow()
			Else
				S46T1.InsertRow(1)
				satirNo = 1
			End if
			S46T1.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		
			S46T1.KOD = StokKodu
			S46T1.TALEPNO = KTTS47T2.EVRAKNO(j)
			S46T1.TALEPARTNO = Trim(KTTS47T2.EVRAKNO(j))&Trim(KTTS47T2.ALAN13(j))
			S46T1.SF_MIKTAR = SiparisMiktari
			S46T1.SF_SF_UNIT = StokBirimi
		Next
		Doc1.Save_Voucher
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Next
	
	Set KTTS47T2 = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_S47T2")
	KayitSayisi = KTTS47T2.GetRecCount
	If KayitSayisi > 0 Then
		For p = 1 To KayitSayisi
			TALEBINARTNOSU = Trim(KTTS47T2.ALAN18(p))
			Set S46T1 = Doc.Select1("fdfsdfs", "STOK46T1", "TALEPARTNO='"&TALEBINARTNOSU&"'", "", -1)
			KaySay = S46T1.GetRecCount
			If KaySay > 0 Then
				SIPARTNO = Trim(S46T1.ARTNO(1))
				KTTS47T2.ALAN12(p) = SIPARTNO
				SIPNO = Trim(S46T1.EVRAKNO(1))
				KTTS47T2.ALAN11(p) = SIPNO
				KTTS47T2.ALAN17(p) = "E"
			End If
		Next
	End If	

	nret = Doc.Save_Voucher
	If nret = 0 Then
		Msgbox "Sipariþe Aktarýldý, Talep Son Haliyle Kaydedildi."
	End If
End Sub
'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub AktarilanlariIptalEt
	Set S47E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK47E")	
	EvrakNo = Trim(S47E.EVRAKNO)
	
	Set KTTS47T2_4 = Doc.Select1("rereref", "KTT_S47T2", "EVRAKNO='"&EvrakNo&"'&ALAN17='H'", "", -1)
	If KTTS47T2_4.GetRecCount = 0 Then Exit Sub
		
	'Kalip Zamani Gelenleri atacaðýmýz boþ bir tablo oluþturuyoruz.
	Set STACK_KTT_S47T2 = Doc.Select1("fdfsd56fsdf", "KTT_S47T2", "ALAN1='______9999zzzzjjjj____'", "", -1)

	'Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=KTT_S47T2","TMPCOPY"
	'Set STACK_KTT_S47T2 = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")
	
	Set KTTS47T2 = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_S47T2")
	KTTS47T2.SetRowAID 1,1,1	

	KayitSayisi = KTTS47T2.GetRecCount
	Alanlar = KTTS47T2.GetFieldNames("")
	
	If KayitSayisi > 0 Then
		For t = 1 To KayitSayisi	
			If Trim(KTTS47T2.ALAN17(t)) = "E" Then
				satirNo = STACK_KTT_S47T2.AddRow
				For each alan in Alanlar
					alanDegeri = KTTS47T2.GetFieldValue(Alan, t)
					STACK_KTT_S47T2.SetFieldValue alan, satirNo, alanDegeri
				Next
			End If
		Next
	End If
	
	KTTS47T2.Empty
	
	Stack_KayitSayisi = STACK_KTT_S47T2.GetRecCount
	If Stack_KayitSayisi = 0 Then Exit Sub

	Alanlar = KTTS47T2.GetFieldNames("")

	For j = 1 to Stack_KayitSayisi
		satirNo = KTTS47T2.AddRow
		For each alan in Alanlar
			alanDegeri = STACK_KTT_S47T2.GetFieldValue(Alan, j)
			KTTS47T2.SetFieldValue alan, satirNo, alanDegeri
		Next
	Next
	
	'nret = Doc.Save_Voucher
	'If nret = 0 Then
	'	Msgbox "Tablo Kaydedilerek Güncellendi."
	'End If
	KTTS47T2.SetRowAID 0,0,0	
End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub EvrakAc()
	Set KTTS47T2 = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_S47T2")
	EvrakNumarasi = Trim(KTTS47T2.ALAN11)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentUI("STOK46", "")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher EvrakNumarasi
End Sub

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DUNDAR_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : MPSSÝL
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : "P" STATÜSÜNDEKÝ ÝÞ EMÝRLERÝNÝ (LÝSTE TAB INDAKÝ) SÝLER.
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	If Listet.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	If Trim(Listet.STATUS)="P" Then
		For i = 1 To Listet.GetRowCount()
			Doc.Load_Voucher Listet.EVRAKNO(i)
			Doc.Delete_Voucher
		Next
	End If
	Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

Sub EvrakAc()
	Set KTTST2CAR = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_ST2CAR")
	SatirNo=KTTST2CAR.GetCurrentRow()
	CariKodu = Trim(KTTST2CAR.ALAN1(SatirNo))
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentUI("CARI00", "")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher CariKodu
End Sub
'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Revizyon
	If Not OzelHakVarMi("REVNO") Then 
		Msgbox "Bu Özelliði Kullanma Yetkiniz Yok."
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
	Set S00 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK00")
	StokKodu = Trim(S00.KOD)
	If StokKodu = "" Then
		Msgbox "Stok Kodu Seçilmemiþ"
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "FORM_REVIZYON", SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, Par
End Sub
'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub RevizyonlariOlustur
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	If Trim(MTable.REVNO) = "" Then
		Msgbox "Revizyon No Girilmemiþ."
		Exit Sub
	Else
		RevizyonNo = Trim(MTable.REVNO)
	End If
	
	Set S00 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK00")
	StokKodu = Trim(S00.KOD)
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK00", "")
	Set S00X = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK00")
	
	YeniStokKartiKodu = StokKodu & "-" & RevizyonNo
	Set S00Kontrol = Doc.Select1("xddd", "STOK00", "KOD='"&YeniStokKartiKodu&"'", "", -1)
	If S00Kontrol.GetRecCount > 0 Then
		Msgbox "Stok Kartý Mevcut. Ýþlem Ýptal Edilmiþtir."
		Exit Sub
	Else
	
		k=doc.d7msgbox("Yeni Stok Kartý : " & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & YeniStokKartiKodu & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Koduyla Açýlacaktýr. Emin Misiniz?", "ONAY", MB_YESNO)
		If k=6 Then
			Doc.RunCommand "MT_DOC_COPY"
			Doc1.Load_Voucher YeniStokKartiKodu
			Doc1.RunCommand "MT_DOC_PASTE"
			ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
			If ret = 0 Then 
				Doc.d7MsgBox "[" & YeniStokKartiKodu & "] Kodlu Stok Kartý Oluþturuldu.", "Ýþlem Tamamlandý", MB_OK
				Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
				'Ürün Aðacý Oluþturma
				Set B01E = Doc.Select1("xxssdd", "BOMU01E", "MAMULCODE='"&StokKodu&"'", "", -1)
				If B01E.GetRecCount = 0 Then
					Msgbox "Ürün Aðacý Yok. Ýþlem Kesilmiþtir."
					Exit Sub
				Else
					EskiUrunAgaciFoyNo = Trim(B01E.EVRAKNO)
					Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("BOMU01", "")
					Set B01E = Doc1.GetTableObject("BOMU01E")
					Set B01T = Doc1.GetTableObject("BOMU01T")
					
					Doc1.Load_Voucher EskiUrunAgaciFoyNo
					Doc1.RunCommand "MT_DOC_COPY"
					Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
					
					Doc1.New_Voucher 
					YeniUrunAgaciFoyNo = Trim(B01E.EVRAKNO)
					Doc1.RunCommand "MT_DOC_PASTE"
					B01E.MAMULCODE = YeniStokKartiKodu
					ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
					If ret = 0 Then 
						Doc.d7MsgBox "[" & YeniStokKartiKodu & "] Koduna ait Ürün Aðacý Aþaðýdaki Yeni Föy No ile Oluþturuldu." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & YeniUrunAgaciFoyNo, "Ýþlem Tamamlandý", MB_OK
						Doc1.Load_Voucher EskiUrunAgaciFoyNo
						B01E.AKTIF_PASIF = " "
						Doc1.Save_Voucher
						Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
					Else
						Doc.d7MsgBox "[" & YeniStokKartiKodu & "]" & " Koduna ait Ürün Aðacý Oluþturulamadý", "Ýþlem Kesilmiþtir.", MB_OK
					End If
					Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
				End If
			Else
				Doc.d7MsgBox "[" & YeniStokKartiKodu & "]" & " Kodlu Stok Kartý Oluþturulamadý", "Ýþlem Kesilmiþtir.", MB_OK
			End If
			Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
			Doc.RUN_APP_EXIT_ONIDLE
		Else
			Exit Sub
		End If
	End If
End Sub
'---------------------------------------
'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function OzelHakVarMi(HakKodu)
	OzelHakVarMi = False
	Set U12 = Doc.Select1("de443d44t", "UDEFT12", "KOD='" & Doc.Getd7UserName() & "'&OZEL_HAK_KODU='" & HakKodu & "'", "", -1)
	if U12.GetRowCount() > 0 Then OzelHakVarMi = True
End Function

Glo_path="C:\Users\esktop\beyaz puantaj.XLS"
Glo_satirsayisi = 100

Function xCdbl(dval)
	Dim xdbl
	xdbl=""&dval
	xdbl=Trim(xdbl)
	if Trim(xdbl)="" then
		xCdbl=0
		Exit Function
	end if
	if instr(xdbl,",")>0 then
		xdbl=RePlace(xdbl,",",".")
	end if
	xCdbl=Doc.Cdbl(xdbl)
End Function

Sub Makro1()
'	on error resume next

	dim strFpath,intSatirSayisi

	Fpath = Glo_path
	strFpath = inputbox("Excel dosyasýnýn yolunu giriniz","Puantaj dosyasýnýn yolu",Glo_path)

	if trim(strFpath)="" Then 
		msgbox("Boþ dosya adý olamaz.Makroyu tekrar çalýþtýrýn.")
		exit Sub
	End if

	intSatirSayisi = inputbox("Excel dosyasýndan okunacak satýr sayýsý","Excel'deki satýr sayýsý",Glo_satirsayisi)

	if not isNumeric(intSatirSayisi) Then 
		msgbox("Boþ ya da yanlýþ satýr bildirimi.Makroyu tekrar çalýþtýrýn.")
		exit Sub
	End if

	dim objExcel
	Set objExcel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")

	dim objWorkbooks
	Set objWorkbooks = objExcel.Workbooks

	


	objWorkbooks.Open(strFpath)
	
	dim objSheet1, sutun
	Set objSheet1 = objWorkbooks(1).Worksheets(1)
	dim c,strPersNo
	dim dblNormalCalisma,dblHaftatatili,dblGenelTatil,dblYillikizin,dblucretliizin,dblGenelTatil1
	dim dblIstirahat,dblucretsiz,dbl100mesai,dbl50mesai,dbldevamsiz,dbleksik,dblmazaret1,dblmazaret2
	dim dblIstirahat2, dblIstirahat1
	if err.number<>0 Then 
		objWorkbooks(1).Close
		objExcel.Quit
		Set objExcel=Nothing
		Set objWorkbooks=Nothing
		Set objSheet1=Nothing
		Exit Sub
	End if	

	For c=1 to BRDR1PBGIRIS.GetRecCount
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Puantaj Bilgileri Aktarýlýyor : " & c & " / " & BRDR1PBGIRIS.GetRecCount

		SicilNo = BRDR1PBGIRIS.C_KOD(c)
		PdksNo = doc.getdbfieldvalue("PERSM0","PDKSNO",SicilNo,"")
		strPersNo = trim(PdksNo)

		BRDR1PBGIRIS.SetCurrentRow(c)

'		if not (xlsCell is Nothing) Then
		if TRUE Then
			sutun=1
			For ik=2 to Glo_satirsayisi
				if Trim(objSheet1.Cells(ik,sutun))=trim(strPersNo) Then
					satir = ik
					Exit For
				End if
			Next

			strPersNoExcel = objSheet1.Cells(satir,sutun).Value
			i = satir
	
			if trim(strPersNoExcel) <> "" Then
	
				if Trim(strPersNoExcel)=Trim(strPersNo) Then

					'NORMAL ÇALIÞMA (saat)
					'if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,2).Value) Then
					'	dblNormalCalisma=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,2).Value)
					'Else
					'	dblNormalCalisma=xCdbl(0)
					'End if

           'NORMAL ÇALIÞMA1 (gün)
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,3).Value) Then
						dblNormalCalisma1=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,3).Value)
					Else
						dblNormalCalisma1=xCdbl(0)
					End if

					'dblNormalCalisma1=Doc.div(dblNormalCalisma,7.5)
					dblNormalCalisma1=Doc.dround(dblNormalCalisma1)
					dblNormalCalisma1 = Doc.Round(dblNormalCalisma1,0)

					'HAFTA TATÝLÝ (SAAT)
					'if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,4).Value) Then
					'	dblHaftatatili=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,4).Value)
					'Else
					'	dblhaftatatili=xCdbl(0)
					'End if
             
            'HAFTA TATÝLÝ1 (gün)
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,11).Value) Then
						dblHaftatatili1=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,11).Value)
					Else
						dblhaftatatili1=xCdbl(0)
					End if
            					
					'GENEL TATÝL (SAAT)
					'if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,12).Value) Then
					'	dblGenelTatil=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,12).Value)
					'Else
					'	dblGenelTatil=xCdbl(0)
					'End if
					'GENEL TATÝL (GÜN)
					'if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,13).Value) Then
					'	dblGenelTatil1=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,13).Value)
					'Else
					'	dblGenelTatil1=xCdbl(0)
					'End if

					'YILLIK IZIN (saat)
					'if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,14).Value) Then
					'	dblYillikizin=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,14).Value)
					'Else
					'	dblYillikizin=xCdbl(0)
					'End if
					'YILLIK IZIN1 (GÜN)
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,15).Value) Then
						dblYillikizin1=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,15).Value)
					Else
						dblYillikizin1=xCdbl(0)
					End if
           'ÜCRETLÝ ÝZÝN (saat)
					'if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,16).Value) Then
					'	dblucretliizin=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,16).Value)
					'Else
					'	dblucretliizin=xCdbl(0)
					'End if
					'ÜCRETLÝ ÝZÝN (GÜN)
					'if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,16).Value) Then
					'	dblucretliizin1=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,16).Value)
					'Else
					'	dblucretliizin1=xCdbl(0)
					'End if
				'	dblucretliizin1=Doc.div(dblucretliizin,7.5)
				'	dblucretliizin1=Doc.dround(dblucretliizin1)

           'DEVAMSIZ(SAAT)
					'if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,6).Value) Then
					'	dbldevamsiz=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,6).Value)
					'Else
					'	dbldevamsiz=xCdbl(0)
					'End if
			    'DEVAMSIZ(GÜN)
					'if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,5).Value) Then
					'	dbldevamsiz1=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,5).Value)
					'Else
					'	dbldevamsiz1=xCdbl(0)
					'End if
				'	dbldevamsiz1=Doc.div(dbldevamsiz,7.5)
				'	dbldevamsiz1=Doc.dround(dbldevamsiz1)

        		'EKSÝK
					'if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,7).Value) Then
					'	dbleksik = xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,7).Value)
					'Else
					'	dbleksik = xCdbl(0)
					'End if
					'ÜCRETSÝZ ÝZÝN(SAAT)
					'if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,18).Value) Then
					'	dblucretsiz = xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,18).Value)
					'Else
					'	dblucretsiz = xCdbl(0)
					'End if
					'ÜCRETSÝZ ÝZÝN (GÜN)
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,19).Value) Then
						dblucretsiz1 = xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,19).Value)
					Else
						dblucretsiz1 = xCdbl(0)
					End if
				'	dblucretsiz1=Doc.div(dblucretsiz,7.5)
				'	dblucretsiz1=Doc.dround(dblucretsiz1)
          
            'ISTIRAHAT1(SAAT)
				  ' if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,20).Value) Then
					'	  dblIstirahat = xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,20).Value)
					'Else
					'	dblIstirahat = xCdbl(0)
					'End if
				   
  	          'VIZITE(GÜN)
					'if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,21).Value) Then
					'	dblIstirahat1 = xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,21).Value)
					'Else
					'	dblIstirahat1 = xCdbl(0)
					'End if

           'ISTIRAHAT2(SAAT)
					'if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,22).Value) Then
					'	dblIstirahat2 = xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,22).Value)
					'Else
					'	dblIstirahat2 = xCdbl(0)
					'End if
           'ISTIRAHAT2(GÜN)
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,23).Value) Then
						dblIstirahat3 = xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,23).Value)
					Else
						dblIstirahat3= xCdbl(0)
					End if
            
					'GOREV
					'if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,25).Value) Then
					'	dblmazaret2 = xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,25).Value)
					'Else
					'	dblmazaret2 = xCdbl(0)
					'End if
					'%50 GUVENLIK MESAI (SAAT)
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,8).Value) Then
						dbl50mesai=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,8).Value)
					Else
						dbl50mesai=xCdbl(0)
					End if
					'%100 GUVENLIK MESAI(SAAT)
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,9).Value) Then 
						dbl100mesai=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,9).Value)
					Else
						dbl100mesai=xCdbl(0)
					End if
				'%50 NORMAL MESAI (SAAT)
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,6).Value) Then
						dbl50mesainormal=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,6).Value)
					Else
						dbl50mesainormal=xCdbl(0)
					End if
					'%100NORMAL MESAI(SAAT)
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,7).Value) Then 
						dbl100mesainormal=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,7).Value)
					Else
						dbl100mesainormal=xCdbl(0)
					End if


' -------------------------------------------------
				'	SskToplami = 0
				'	SskToplami = BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG01(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG02(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG03(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG04(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG05(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KIG01(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KIG02(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KIG06(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KIG07(c)
				'	If SskToplami>=31 then
				'		BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG02(c) = BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG02(c) - 1
				'	end if
' -------------------------------------------------

					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AG_KCG01(c) = dblNormalCalisma1
          	BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AG_KCG02(c) = dblHaftatatili1
          	'BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AG_KCG03(c) = dblGenelTatil1
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AG_KCG04(c) = dblYillikizin1
					'BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AG_KCG05(c) = dblucretliizin1
					'BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AG_KCG06(c) = dblIstirahat1
					'BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AG_KCG07(c) = dblmazaret2
          	BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AG_KIG01(c) = dblIstirahat3
            BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AG_KIG02(c) = dblucretsiz1
				'	BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AG_KIG07(c) = dbldevamsiz1
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_E_AS_E01(c) = dbl50mesai
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_E_AS_E02(c) = dbl100mesai
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_E_AS_E03(c) = dbl50mesainormal
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_E_AS_E04(c) = dbl100mesainormal
				
					SskToplami = 0
					SskToplami = BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG01(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG02(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG04(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KIG01(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KIG02(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KIG06(c)
					If SskToplami>=31 then
						BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG02(c) = BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG02(c) - 1
					end if
			
				End if
			End if

			if False Then 
				MSGBOX "Hata " & Err.Number & " " & Error()
				objWorkbooks(1).Close
				objExcel.Quit
				Exit Sub
				Set objExcel=Nothing
				Set objWorkbooks=Nothing
				Set objSheet1=Nothing
			End if	
		End if
		if False Then 
			objWorkbooks(1).Close
			objExcel.Quit
			Set objExcel=Nothing
			Set objWorkbooks=Nothing
			Set objSheet1=Nothing
			Exit Sub
		End if	
	Next
	
	objWorkbooks(1).Close
	objExcel.Quit

	Set objExcel=Nothing
	Set objWorkbooks=Nothing
	Set objSheet1=Nothing
	msgbox "Dosya aktarma iþlemi bitti."
End Sub

Function xCdbl(dval)
	Dim xdbl
	xdbl=""&dval
	xdbl=Trim(xdbl)
	if Trim(xdbl)="" then
		xCdbl=0
		Exit Function
	end if
	if instr(xdbl,",")>0 then
		xdbl=RePlace(xdbl,",",".")
	end if
	xCdbl=Doc.Cdbl(xdbl)
End Function

Sub Makro1()
'	on error resume next

	dim strFpath,intSatirSayisi

	Fpath = Glo_path
	strFpath = inputbox("Excel dosyasýnýn yolunu giriniz","Puantaj dosyasýnýn yolu",Glo_path)

	if trim(strFpath)="" Then 
		msgbox("Boþ dosya adý olamaz.Makroyu tekrar çalýþtýrýn.")
		exit Sub
	End if

	intSatirSayisi = inputbox("Excel dosyasýndan okunacak satýr sayýsý","Excel'deki satýr sayýsý",Glo_satirsayisi)

	if not isNumeric(intSatirSayisi) Then 
		msgbox("Boþ ya da yanlýþ satýr bildirimi.Makroyu tekrar çalýþtýrýn.")
		exit Sub
	End if

	dim objExcel
	Set objExcel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")

	dim objWorkbooks
	Set objWorkbooks = objExcel.Workbooks

	


	objWorkbooks.Open(strFpath)
	
	dim objSheet1, sutun
	Set objSheet1 = objWorkbooks(1).Worksheets(1)
	dim c,strPersNo
	dim dblNormalCalisma,dblHaftatatili,dblGenelTatil,dblYillikizin,dblucretliizin,dblGenelTatil1
	dim dblIstirahat,dblucretsiz,dbl100mesai,dbl50mesai,dbldevamsiz,dbleksik,dblmazaret1,dblmazaret2
	dim dblIstirahat2, dblIstirahat1
	if err.number<>0 Then 
		objWorkbooks(1).Close
		objExcel.Quit
		Set objExcel=Nothing
		Set objWorkbooks=Nothing
		Set objSheet1=Nothing
		Exit Sub
	End if	

	For c=1 to BRDR1PBGIRIS.GetRecCount
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Puantaj Bilgileri Aktarýlýyor : " & c & " / " & BRDR1PBGIRIS.GetRecCount

		SicilNo = BRDR1PBGIRIS.C_KOD(c)
		PdksNo = doc.getdbfieldvalue("PERSM0","PDKSNO",SicilNo,"")
		strPersNo = trim(PdksNo)

		BRDR1PBGIRIS.SetCurrentRow(c)

'		if not (xlsCell is Nothing) Then
		if TRUE Then
			sutun=1

Sub IseGirisBilgileriDoldur(ByVal ExcelSheet,ByVal Satir,ByVal PersonelNo)
	Dim Tbl_SRVPC0U
	Set Tbl_SRVPC0U=Doc.GetTableObject("SRVPC0U")

Sub Makro1()
	Dim myExcel
	Dim mySheet
	Set myExcel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")

	myExcel.Workbooks.Open ("D:\KALIP.XLS")
	Set mySheet = myExcel.Workbooks("KALIP.XLS").Worksheets("KALIP")

	Dim PersonelNo

	Dim I		
	For I=2 To 170
		PersonelNo=Trim(mySheet.Cells(I, 1))
		If PersonelNo<>"" Then
			PersonelNo=Doc.LeftJustify(PersonelNo,16)
			Doc.Load_Voucher PersonelNo
			If Trim(Doc.GetLoadedVoucher())=Trim(PersonelNo) Then
				Call IseGirisBilgileriDoldur(mySheet,I,PersonelNo)
				Doc.Save_Voucher
			Else
				MsgBox(Trim(PersonelNo) & " Nolu Personel Bulunamadý")
			End If
		End If
	Next

	myExcel.Quit

	Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("D:\KALIP.xls")
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)

	Set SVPC0 = Doc.GetTableObject("SRVPC0")
	Set SVPC0U = Doc.GetTableObject("SRVPC0U")
	Set ddd = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	For i = 2 to 65000
		KalipKodu = Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value
		if trim(KalipKodu) = "" Then Exit For
		KalipAd = trim(Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value)
		kalipsinifi = trim(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value)
		ukoddeger = trim(Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value)
		
		Doc.Load_Voucher KalipKodu
		SVPC0.AD1 = KalipAd
		SVPC0.SINIFI = kalipsinifi

		SatirNo = SVPC0U.Addrow
		SVPC0U.SetCurrentRow(SatirNo)
		SVPC0U.UKOD = ukoddeger
		
		Doc.Save_Voucher

	Next
	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
End Sub
